byte-level BPE tokenizer 在英语主导的数据分布下，会系统性地把西里尔文字语言切得更碎，导致同样语义内容需要更多 token。论文不只停留在“乌克兰语更贵”这个现象层面，而是进一步区分了三件事：一是 UTF-8 字节长度带来的先天不利；二是训练语料中脚本和语言配比导致的 merge 学习不足；三是这种 token 开销如何传导到上下文容量、推理成本和真实应用效果。作者想回答的核心不是单纯测量 fertility，而是：这种开销到底有多大、是否主要由训练分布而非编码本身决定、以及在不改模型和改 tokenizer 两条路上分别能缓解多少。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在 tokenizer 与数据配比，而不是下游应用。文章给出的结论很清楚：西里尔语言的 tokenization 劣势并不只是“字符占两个字节”这么简单，训练语料中对该脚本分配不足，会进一步放大 merge 稀缺，最终形成成本和上下文窗口上的结构性不平等。这个判断对多语种 tokenizer 设计、语料配比、词表预算分配都有直接启发。更重要的是，作者用受控实验说明，在 200K 词表上做更平衡的英乌联合训练，可以把 UK/EN token ratio 从 2.22× 压到 1.30×，说明问题相当大一部分是可训练、可修复的，而不是编码层面的硬约束。对做 multilingual pretrain 的人，这比单纯抱怨 fertility 更有用，因为它把问题落到了可操作的 recipe：数据配比、词表预算、脚本覆盖和 tokenizer retraining。","technical_approach":"方法上分成三块。第一块是系统测量。作者在五种有标准化西里尔/拉丁双书写形式的语言上做 cross-script 对照，包括 Ukrainian、Serbian、Kazakh、Azerbaijani、Moldovan，总计 8.37M 词形。核心指标是 corpus-level fertility，以及乌克兰语相对英语的 overhead 比值 R(t)=F(t,BrUK)/F(t,Brown)。这种设计的好处是把“语言本身信息密度”和“脚本/词表适配”尽量拆开：同一语言换脚本后 token 数变化，更接近 tokenizer 对脚本的偏置，而不是语义差异。\n\n第二块是 tokenizer 重训实验。作者没有采用 post-hoc 往现有词表里补字符的路线，因为现代 tokenizer 里西里尔字符本身并不缺，真正缺的是高频子词 merge。于是他们直接训练 balanced byte-level BPE tokenizer，在固定 200K 词表上调节英乌语料配比，观察 fertility 和 UK/EN ratio 的变化。这个设计针对的是 merge 统计学习机制：如果训练语料里乌克兰语片段出现得足够多，BPE 会学到更长、更稳定的西里尔子词，从而降低 token 数。论文强调最佳 balanced tokenizer 实际在 158,184 个条目就收敛，说明不是词表越大越好，而是有效预算如何分配更关键。\n\n第三块是推理时缓解。作者选用 LLMLingua-2 做 prompt compression，因为其 backbone 是 XLM-R，天然支持多语种，不需要为乌克兰语单独微调。这里的定位很克制：prompt compression 不是从根上修 tokenizer 偏置，而是作为 inference-time 补救手段，看看在真实业务 prompt 中能否部分回收上下文和成本损失。论文还加入 romanization 对照，目的不是鼓励改写脚本，而是检验“换成拉丁字母是否天然更省 token”。这一步很关键，因为它进一步支持作者的主张：token 开销不应被简单归因于 UTF-8 两字节，而要看 tokenizer 是否学到了对应脚本的 merge 结构。","experiments":"实验最有信息量的结果有四组。\n\n第一，生产 tokenizer 基准测试。作者评测了 9 个商用 tokenizer，在 BrUK 与 Brown 上计算 fertility。乌克兰语在现代 tokenizer 上相对英语有 68%–121% 的 token overhead，在较老的 cl100k 上达到 220%。这说明问题不是个别模型的偶然现象，而是跨 tokenizer 普遍存在，只是程度不同。\n\n第二，跨语言脚本对照。五种语言的西里尔/拉丁标准写法比较显示，token overhead 会跟该语言在网络和训练语料中的主导书写系统一起变化。这个结果支持“训练分布决定 merge 质量”的解释，而不是把差异全部归结为 Unicode 编码长度。\n\n第三，balanced tokenizer 重训。固定 200K 词表上，英乌平衡训练把 UK/EN ratio 从 2.22× 降到 1.30×，最佳 tokenizer 在 158,184 个实际词表项附近收敛，统计显著性给到 p < 0.0001。这个幅度很大，说明只靠 tokenizer 训练数据重配，就能回收相当一部分上下文浪费。\n\n第四，真实应用验证。作者在 1,536 个真实电商商品样本上做 production-like benchmark，并用确定性准确率评估 prompt compression 等缓解手段。摘要截断没有给出全部数字，但从全文结构看，这部分的作用不是证明压缩方法本身多强，而是验证 corpus-level fertility 差异确实会传导到实际系统成本与可用上下文，并且 inference-time 压缩与 tokenizer 重训是互补而非替代关系。\n\n另外一个值得注意的细节是，作者报告西里尔词表分配与 overhead 呈负相关，Spearman ρ = -0.536，但 p = 0.215，样本数只有 7。也就是说方向上符合直觉，但统计证据还不够硬。这个处理比较诚实，没有把弱相关包装成强结论。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你关心 multilingual 训练设置和语料配比细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a concrete but under-addressed pretraining issue: mainstream multilingual byte-level BPE tokenizers systematically over-segment Ukrainian and other underrepresented Cyrillic-script languages, so the same semantic content consumes many more tokens than English. The contribution is not just another fertility measurement. It tries to separate three factors: the UTF-8 byte disadvantage of Cyrillic, the lack of learned subword merges caused by English-heavy tokenizer training data, and the downstream effect on cost, latency, and usable context length. The central question is whether the overhead is mostly an unavoidable encoding artifact or a fixable consequence of tokenizer training allocation.","importance":"This is relevant to pretraining through tokenizer design and data mixture, not through downstream modeling. The main takeaway is that Cyrillic token inefficiency is not explained by “two bytes per character” alone. Training distribution matters a great deal: if a script receives too little mass during tokenizer training, BPE learns too few useful merges, and the language pays a recurring tax in context budget and inference cost. The strongest evidence is the balanced tokenizer experiment: under a 200K vocabulary cap, the Ukrainian/English token ratio drops from 2.22x to 1.30x. That is large enough to matter for multilingual pretraining recipes. For anyone building multilingual foundation models, this paper turns a fairness complaint into an actionable tokenizer/data-allocation problem.","technical_approach":"$a","experiments":"$b","reading_guide":"$c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做 multilingual pretrain 或 tokenizer 的人该看；结论不新，但数字够硬，能提醒你别再默认英语 token budget 可直接外推到西里尔语。","en":"Worth reading if you work on multilingual pretraining or tokenizers; not a paradigm shift, but the measurements are concrete enough to break the lazy assumption that English token budgets transfer to Cyrillic."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Independent Researcher","Ivan Dobrovolskyi, Independent Researcher"],"authors":["Ivan Dobrovolskyi"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["tokenization","multilingual","Cyrillic","vocabulary-allocation","data-efficiency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21384","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21384","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22179","title":"Token-Level Likelihood-Array Regression for Membership Inference and AI-Generated Text Detection","abstract":"Membership inference asks whether a text was used to train a language model, whereas AI-generated text detection asks whether it was generated by a language model rather than written by a human. Existing likelihood-based methods typically compress token-level probabilities into a few prespecified scores, most often using only probabilities conditioned on the full preceding context. We propose likelihood-array regression (LAR), which evaluates each target token under nested left-context windows a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22179.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.LG","stat.ME"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是基于语言模型 likelihood 的两类审计任务：判断文本是否进入过某个模型的训练集，以及判断文本是否由 AI 生成。现有方法通常把 token-level likelihood 压缩成少数几个手工分数，主要依赖 full left context 下的 next-token log probability。论文的问题是：不同上下文长度、不同 token 位置、不同 likelihood 特征中的检测信号如何被系统建模，而不是提前压缩掉。","importance":"这篇论文和预训练数据治理有关。membership inference 可用于训练数据泄漏、版权语料使用和隐私审计；AI-generated text detection 可用于识别合成数据污染和混合来源语料。对基座模型研究员的启发是，token likelihood 不只是一个平均 loss，短上下文、长上下文和位置相关的 likelihood 变化都可能携带训练记忆或生成痕迹。这个观察也会影响数据去重、contamination 检测、合成数据过滤和模型审计方法的设计。","technical_approach":"论文提出 likelihood-array regression，简称 LAR。它不只计算每个 token 在完整前缀下的 log probability，而是对同一个目标 token 使用一系列嵌套 left-context window 重新打分，得到一个按上下文长度和相对 token 位置索引的 likelihood array。每个 array cell 还包含若干来自预测分布的特征。由于文档长度不同，论文把这些 cell 对齐到公共域上，再做统一回归。LAR-1 学习单个 cell 的位置、上下文尺度和 likelihood 特征对二分类 log odds 的贡献，并在 token 和 context 维度上聚合。LAR-2 进一步加入同一目标 token 的二阶 within-path 特征，包括同一上下文下的二次项，以及不同上下文长度之间的乘积项。实现上使用 basis expansion 和 random squared projection 做有限维近似，再用 ridge logistic regression 估计。理论部分给出 within-path quadratic model 的 minimax 预测界，并把有限维估计误差分解为近似、正则、估计和平方投影误差。","experiments":"论文在 membership inference 和 AI-generated text detection 两类任务上验证 LAR。每类任务覆盖五个 scoring model。membership inference 的平均 held-out AUC 为 0.914–0.983；AI-generated text detection 的平均 held-out AUC 为 0.985–0.996，明显高于已有 likelihood-based 方法。受限 array 分析显示，短上下文 likelihood 提供了 full-context likelihood 之外的信息，这说明训练记忆或生成痕迹不只体现在完整前缀条件下的困惑度。二阶 within-path 特征的增益主要出现在 membership inference，说明同一 token 在不同上下文尺度下的 likelihood 变化模式对训练成员识别更有用。理论上，论文在 product-form covariance decay 假设下给出匹配的 minimax 上下界，速率为 {log(en)/n}^{ν/(ν+1)}，并进一步连接 prediction error 与 AUC 收敛。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–40 分钟。先看方法部分的 likelihood array 构造，确认它和传统 full-context perplexity、loss tail、rank-based detector 的差异。然后看 LAR-1 与 LAR-2 的定义，重点理解为什么二阶项只沿同一个目标 token 的 context path 构造，而不是对所有 token pair 建模；这是控制维度和保留上下文尺度信息之间的折中。实验部分优先看跨 scoring model 的 AUC 表、restricted-array 消融，以及二阶特征对 membership inference 的增益。理论部分可只读结论和假设，重点关注 covariance decay、有限维近似和 AUC convergence 的连接。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses two likelihood-based auditing problems for language models: membership inference, which asks whether a text was used in training a target model, and AI-generated text detection, which asks whether a text was produced by a language model. Existing methods usually compress token-level probabilities into a few handcrafted scores, mostly based on full-prefix next-token likelihoods. The paper asks how to model detection signals that vary jointly with context length, token position, and likelihood-derived features.","importance":"The paper is relevant to pretraining data governance rather than core model training. Membership inference is useful for privacy, copyright, and training-data usage audits. AI-generated text detection is relevant for filtering synthetic contamination and mixed-source corpora. The main lesson for pretraining researchers is that token likelihood should not be treated only as an average loss. Short-context likelihoods, long-context likelihoods, token position, and their interactions can carry different signals about memorization and machine generation.","technical_approach":"$d","experiments":"The experiments cover both membership inference and AI-generated text detection across five scoring models in each setting. LAR achieves mean held-out AUCs of 0.914–0.983 for membership inference and 0.985–0.996 for AI-generated text detection, outperforming existing likelihood-based procedures. Restricted-array analyses show that likelihoods computed with shorter left contexts contain information beyond conventional full-context likelihoods. The within-path second-order augmentation helps mainly in membership inference, suggesting that how a token’s likelihood changes across context scales is informative for detecting training membership. On the theory side, the paper establishes matching minimax lower and upper prediction bounds at the rate {log(en)/n}^{ν/(ν+1)} under product-form covariance decay.","reading_guide":"A 25–40 minute skim is appropriate. Read the likelihood-array construction first, since this is the main difference from full-context perplexity, loss-tail, and rank-based detectors. Then read the definitions of LAR-1 and LAR-2, especially why second-order terms are restricted to the same token’s context path instead of all token pairs; this is the main tradeoff between expressivity and dimensional control. In the experiments, focus on the cross-scoring-model AUC tables, the restricted-array ablations, and the gains from second-order features in membership inference. The theory can be read selectively, with attention to the covariance-decay assumptions, finite-resolution conditions, and the link from prediction error to AUC.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这不是提性能的paper，但对训练数据泄露怎么从token似然里冒出来讲得更细；只看方法和误差界，实验先当弱信号。","en":"Not a capability paper, but it sharpens how training-data leakage surfaces in token likelihoods; read the method and theory, treat the empirical gains as a weaker signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiajun Sun","Zhanrui Cai"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["membership-inference","data-leakage","likelihood","ai-generated-text-detection","token-level-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22179","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22179","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22858","title":"Mapping the Concept Landscape: Structural Perception of Global Distributions for Transparent Data Pruning","abstract":"Existing data pruning methods predominantly rely on high-dimensional feature embeddings to measure sample importance. However, these compressed vectors often obscure fine-grained semantic interactions, leading to suboptimal coverage of rare semantic concepts in the pruned subsets. In this paper, we propose Mapping the Concept Landscape (MCL), a novel structural perception framework for transparent data pruning. Instead of abstract embeddings, we represent each image-caption pair as an explicit s","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22858.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决的是多模态预训练数据裁剪中的语义覆盖问题。现有方法多依赖 CLIP 或类似模型的高维 embedding，用距离、相似度、loss、uncertainty 等信号选样本。问题是 embedding 把一个图文样本压成单个向量，容易把多实体、多事件、多属性的组合语义折叠掉。结果是，裁剪集在向量空间里看起来分散，但可能丢掉低频概念；或者保留了几何上不同、语义上重复的样本。","importance":"对预训练数据研究的启发点在于，它把数据裁剪的单位从“样本向量”改成了“显式概念”。这比单纯换一个采样分数更有信息量。对于图文预训练，数据质量不只取决于 caption 流畅度或图文匹配分数，还取决于概念分布是否覆盖长尾实体、事件和属性。MCL 的贡献量级更接近一种可解释的数据选择框架，而不是一个新的训练目标。它适合启发 web-scale 多模态数据清洗、子集构造、数据审计，也可借鉴到文本预训练中的实体/事件/主题级 coverage 控制。","technical_approach":"MCL 的流程分四步。第一，把每个 image-caption pair 转成 sample-level semantic graph，节点是显式概念，包括 entities、events、attributes，边表示样本内部的语义关系。第二，把所有样本图聚合成 dataset-level concept graph，用全局频率描述概念分布。第三，根据概念频率定义重要性：常见概念的边际价值递减，低频概念在未覆盖时贡献更高。第四，把裁剪写成概念覆盖最大化问题，在预算约束下用 greedy selection 逐步选择边际收益最高的样本。这个设计和 embedding diversity 的主要差别是，优化对象不是样本间几何距离，而是显式概念的覆盖度。它也不是简单过采样长尾，因为覆盖目标是单调且饱和的；同一概念重复出现后边际收益会下降。","experiments":"实验主张是，在图文数据裁剪场景中，MCL 用少于 10% 的训练时间和选中数据量，达到接近 full-data baseline 的表现。消融层面的关键观察应关注三类：显式概念图是否优于 embedding-based selection；稀有概念加权是否比均匀 coverage 更稳；greedy marginal gain 是否能同时减少冗余并保留主干语义。由于提供的全文片段没有给出具体 benchmark、模型规模、数据集名称和完整数值，不能确认各任务的绝对提升幅度。需要注意的是，方法效果会受概念抽取器质量、概念粒度、图构建成本影响；单样本抽取有噪声，但作者的论点是大规模聚合后频率统计会比单点错误更稳定。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 15 到 25 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中的 Embedding–Concept Misalignment 论证，它解释了为什么向量空间多样性不等于语义概念覆盖。然后看 Method 的三层结构：sample-level graph、dataset-level graph、coverage objective。图 1 应该是最快理解流程的入口。实验部分建议重点看与 embedding-based pruning、loss/uncertainty-based pruning 的对比，以及概念频率加权和 greedy 选择的消融。若关心预训练数据工程，可以深挖两个点：概念抽取是否可扩展到亿级样本；概念 taxonomy 的粒度变化会如何影响数据配比。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses semantic coverage in multimodal data pruning. Most existing pruning methods select image-text samples through dense embeddings, loss, uncertainty, difficulty, or influence scores. This compresses a multi-concept sample into a single vector. As a result, a pruned subset may look diverse in embedding space while still dropping rare entities, events, or attributes, or keeping samples that are geometrically different but conceptually redundant.","importance":"The useful idea for pretraining research is the shift from sample-vector pruning to explicit concept-level pruning. For vision-language pretraining, data quality is not only about caption quality or image-text alignment; it also depends on whether the retained subset covers the long tail of concepts. MCL is best viewed as an interpretable data selection framework rather than a new training objective. The same principle could inform web-scale multimodal data filtering, dataset auditing, mixture construction, and possibly text-only pretraining where entity, event, or topic coverage matters.","technical_approach":"MCL first maps each image-caption pair into a sample-level semantic graph. The graph contains explicit concepts such as entities, events, and attributes. These graphs are then aggregated into a dataset-level concept graph that summarizes global concept frequencies. The pruning objective is formulated as concept coverage maximization under a data budget. Frequent concepts have diminishing marginal value, while underrepresented concepts receive higher gain when they are newly covered. A greedy algorithm selects samples by their marginal contribution to this coverage objective. The main distinction from embedding-diversity methods is that MCL optimizes explicit semantic coverage rather than distances in a learned representation space. It also avoids naive long-tail oversampling because the objective is saturating: repeatedly selecting the same concept quickly reduces marginal gain.","experiments":"The reported result is that MCL can use less than 10% of the training time and selected data while reaching performance comparable to full-data baselines in vision-language pruning settings. The most relevant experimental signals are comparisons against embedding-based, loss-based, and uncertainty-based pruning; ablations on concept rarity weighting; and ablations on greedy marginal-gain selection. The provided full-text excerpt does not include exact benchmark names, model sizes, dataset scales, or per-task numbers, so those should be checked in the experimental tables. A practical caveat is that the framework depends on concept extraction quality, concept granularity, and graph construction cost. The authors’ argument is that individual extraction noise becomes less harmful after aggregation over the dataset.","reading_guide":"Skim time: 15 to 25 minutes. How low a loss the same compute can ultimately reach depends on how each step's gradient is converted into a weight update; the rule that performs this conversion is the optimizer. From SGD and AdamW to the recent Muon, effective update rules have mostly been shaped by engineering intuition and then selected on benchmarks. Muon semi-orthogonalizes the momentum matrix before applying the update and has kept breaking records on public training benchmarks; ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22994.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决两个一直主要靠经验调参的问题：为什么 Muon 里“先对 momentum 矩阵做半正交化再更新”会有效，以及 momentum 的时间常数到底该取多长。作者没有把优化器继续当作经验规则堆叠，而是把训练中的权重矩阵视为一个“受力后会响应、且内部应力会滞后释放”的介质，试图从物理响应模型直接推出更新方向和记忆结构。更具体地说，论文把梯度看成外力，把参数更新看成介质在安全约束下的耗散响应，把 momentum 看成介质内部应力的历史积累。这样一来，Muon 的正交化方向不再只是工程技巧，而被解释为在输出侧安全预算约束下的最大耗散响应；而 momentum 不再只是单一 EMA，而应由介质的应力松弛谱决定，真实介质通常不止一个时间尺度，因此单时间常数未必是合适近似。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一层是方法层：它给 Muon 这类近年表现很强、但解释相对薄弱的优化器补上了一个可推导的理论框架，而且这个框架不是事后拟合某个公式，而是直接导向了一个新优化器 Bi-Maxwell。第二层是研究范式层：它把“优化器设计”从经验搜索往“由训练动力学假设推出算法结构”推进了一步。对于大模型预训练，这种工作的重要性不在于某个 benchmark 快了几十步本身，而在于它提出了一个可测量、可随训练阶段变化的量——最优记忆长度。这个量如果成立，会直接影响 warmup 之后的 β 调度、不同层/不同矩阵的时间常数设计，以及后续把 optimizer state 做成更细粒度动态系统的可能性。贡献量级上，我会把它看成‘对 Muon 家族有实质推进、对更广义优化器研究提供了一个值得验证的建模框架’，不是已经定论的通用答案，但明显超过普通小修小补。","technical_approach":"技术路线分成两部分。第一部分是从响应模型推导 Muon 方向。作者把权重矩阵的更新写成一个受约束的响应问题：在给定输出侧安全预算下，寻找能最大化耗散的响应方向。这里“安全预算”约束的是更新对输出的影响不能过大，而“最大耗散”对应的是在当前受力下尽可能有效地消耗能量。沿这个思路，最优响应会落到 momentum 矩阵的正交极分解方向，也就是 Muon 使用的 semi-orthogonalized update。换句话说，Muon 不是在做逐元素自适应缩放，而是在矩阵几何上选择一个谱范数意义下更稳、更能保持各向同性推进的方向。它和 AdamW 的区别也在这里：AdamW 主要是坐标级别的二阶统计预条件；Muon 更像是在矩阵层面控制更新的形状，避免某些奇异值方向过强、某些方向过弱。\n\n第二部分是从介质应力松弛推导双时间尺度 momentum。经典 momentum 默认只有一个指数衰减时间常数，相当于假设内部应力只有单一松弛模式。作者认为这对真实训练动力学过于粗糙，于是借用 Maxwell 型粘弹模型，把内部应力分解为快、慢两个分量，各自用不同 β 追踪，再组合成最终用于 Muon 正交化的 momentum。算法上，Bi-Maxwell 尽量保持与 Muon 兼容：正交化、其余训练 recipe、甚至 benchmark 环境都不改，只替换单一 EMA 为双 EMA 记忆。这样做的好处是因果关系更清楚，性能变化更容易归因于“记忆结构”而不是别的工程细节。\n\n从全文表述看，作者还强调一个关键点：最优记忆长度不是常数，而应随训练阶段变化。早期梯度方向变化快，长记忆会把过时方向拖进来；后期方向更稳定，长记忆能更好降噪并积累一致信号。Bi-Maxwell 不是显式做时间调度，而是用两个固定时间尺度去近似一个随阶段变化的有效记忆长度，这比手工 schedule 更稳，也更容易直接落地。","experiments":"实验最关键的是两个结果。第一，Bi-Maxwell 在 modded-nanogpt 这个公开 optimizer speedrun benchmark 上，把到达目标 loss 的步数做到 2635 步，超过当时公开记录 2690 步；如果只和不带 SOAP 等附加组件的 tuned-Muon baseline 比，也提前了 40 步到达目标。这个提升幅度不算巨大，但在一个已经被社区长期对抗式调参的公开赛道里，能只改 momentum 结构就继续前推，说明信号不是噪声级别。\n\n第二，作者做了 8 条独立训练轨迹上的只读测量，去估计训练过程中“最优 momentum 记忆长度”的变化。结论是早期最优记忆短，后期逐渐变长，与他们的应力松弛解释一致。这个实验的价值不在于绝对数值，而在于它把一个过去通常只能靠 sweep β 间接猜的超参数，变成了可以沿训练过程被观测和讨论的对象。\n\n从摘要和正文可见，论文还强调比较的公平性：不是作者自己搭了一个容易赢的 baseline，而是拿公开 benchmark 上已经被反复调过的 standing record 做对照。对优化器论文来说，这一点很重要，因为很多增益都可能来自 recipe 漂移、实现细节或评测口径变化。这里作者通过“保持 Muon 其他组件不变，只替换单时间尺度 momentum”为双时间尺度，尽量把改进归因收紧到核心假设。\n\n目前公开内容里没有看到大规模到数十亿参数的完整新训练曲线，只提到 Muon 家族本身已被扩展到 billion 级和 trillion-token 级训练。也就是说，这篇论文的直接实证仍主要集中在公开小型 speedrun 环境；它更像是‘理论驱动的小规模强验证’，而不是已经完成大规模工业复现的 recipe 定稿。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和作者对问题的 framing，确认他们不是在重新包装 momentum，而是在重新定义“更新方向”和“记忆”各自对应的物理对象。然后重点看第 3 节，尤其是 3.2 附近关于内部应力、松弛谱和记忆长度的推导，这是全文真正有新意的地方。看这部分时要抓住两个问题：一，输出侧安全预算具体约束了什么，因此为什么会导向极分解/半正交化方向；二，为什么单一 EMA 对应的是过强的单模态松弛假设，而双时间尺度是最小但合理的扩展。\n\n实验部分优先看 benchmark 主结果图和“最优记忆长度”随训练步变化的测量图。前者回答‘方法有没有用’，后者回答‘理论有没有被数据支持’。如果时间有限，可以先不深究所有物理类比是否严格成立，而是重点检查：推导里哪些假设是必要的，哪些只是为了得到闭式形式；Appendix F 讨论了假设边界和放松方式，这部分值得看，因为它决定这个框架能否迁移到非矩阵参数、不同层类型或更复杂 optimizer 设计。\n\n精读时还可以带着两个延伸问题。第一，Bi-Maxwell 只是两个时间尺度，是否可以自然推广到连续谱或按层自适应谱；第二，既然最优记忆长度随阶段变化，是否可以直接在线估计并调 β，而不是用双 EMA 近似。论文给了方向，但没有把这些问题做完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles two optimizer questions that have mostly been answered by tuning rather than by theory: why Muon’s semi-orthogonalized momentum direction works, and how long momentum should remember past gradients. Instead of treating the optimizer as an empirical update rule, the authors model the weight matrix as a responsive medium with internal memory. Gradients act as external forces, parameter updates are the medium’s dissipative response under a safety constraint, and momentum is interpreted as accumulated internal stress rather than particle-like inertia. Under this view, Muon’s update direction is no longer an engineering trick: it becomes the maximally dissipative response under an output-side safety budget. Likewise, a single EMA is no longer the natural default for memory, because real stress relaxation is typically multi-timescale rather than single-timescale.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is twofold. First, it gives the Muon family a more principled foundation and uses that foundation to derive a new optimizer, Bi-Maxwell, rather than merely rationalizing an existing heuristic after the fact. Second, it reframes optimizer design as a problem of modeling training dynamics and deriving algorithmic structure from those assumptions. For large-scale pretraining, the most interesting contribution is not the benchmark win by itself, but the introduction of an interpretable quantity: the optimal momentum memory length, which is argued to vary over training. If that picture holds more broadly, it has direct implications for beta scheduling, layer- or tensor-specific memory design, and more dynamic optimizer-state parameterizations. I would place the contribution as a meaningful advance for Muon-style optimizers and a credible modeling proposal for broader optimizer research, though not yet a settled universal theory.","technical_approach":"$e","experiments":"$f","reading_guide":"$10","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"为近期极火的 Muon 优化器提供了物理学视角的理论背书，解释了半正交化的合理性，对理解和改进新型优化器极具启发，必读其理论推导部分。","en":"Provides a physics-based theoretical backing for the highly popular Muon optimizer, explaining the rationality of semi-orthogonalization. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22322.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是训练内存里一个常被简化的问题：优化器状态量化不能只按“Adam 的 dense 一阶/二阶矩”来设计，因为现代省内存优化器已经改变了状态的拓扑和它进入更新公式的方式。Adafactor 的二阶矩是分解的行列统计，CAME 额外维护 confidence states，APOLLO 则把统计量放到投影梯度空间里。相同大小的状态重建误差，在这些优化器里会通过完全不同的路径放大成更新误差。论文要解决的核心问题，就是如何为这些非 这类结构化状态优化器，单纯压缩 dense Adam states 已经覆盖不了真实训练栈。论文给出的启发很明确：量化方案必须同时考虑三件事——状态表示的数值分布、状态张量的拓扑结构、以及该状态如何进入 update operator。这个视角对做大规模预训练 recipe、内存受限训练、低成本持续预训练的人都很重要，因为它说明“省内存”不是只看状态字节数，还要看量化误差会不会在更新规则里被放大。贡献量级上，它更像一篇扎实的训练系统/优化方法论文，而不是改变训练范式的工作，但问题选得准，分析也比较到位。","technical_approach":"方法核心是提出 Adaptive Log-Space, AL 量化，并把它放到一个更广的分析框架里。作者先指出，很多优化器状态，尤其二阶矩和 confidence 这类非负 EMA，具有长尾、跨 block 动态范围差异大、同时又包含大量精确零值的特征。若直接用常规线性 block quantization，容易把小正值压成零，破坏“有历史”和“无历史”的语义区别。AL 的做法是：对每个 block 的正值自适应拟合一个对数区间，用 log-space codebook 表示非零部分，同时保留 code zero 专门对应 exact zero，从而满足 q=0 当且仅当 x=0 的双向精确零不变性。这样 dormant states 不会被伪造出正历史，正的二阶矩也不会因为量化塌缩成零。论文没有把所有状态一刀切成同一精度，而是采用 state-specific policy：非负状态用 AL8 或 AL16，带符号的一阶动量单独编码，CAME 的 confidence states 可以使用比二阶矩更高的精度。另一个关键点是 topology-aware grouping。作者反复强调，量化 block 的边界若切穿 Adafactor 的行/列因子，或者把不相关统计量混在一起，会显著放大 update error；因此 block granularity 和 parameter grouping 必须跟状态拓扑对齐。分析对象覆盖四条路径：dense AdamW、factored Adafactor、带 confidence 的 CAME、以及 projected-state 的 APOLLO。评估指标也不是停留在状态重建误差，而是进一步看 update-domain fidelity、temporal drift，以及量化误差经过 inverse square root、factor reconstruction、confidence modulation、projected scaling 后的传播。实现上，作者同时给了 Python reference 和 fused CUDA path，并单独验证 reference 算法一致性与 CUDA 数值一致性，这一点对训练系统论文是加分项。","experiments":"实验分成诊断和端到端两层。诊断层面，作者先从语言模型预训练轨迹里抽取不同优化器状态，比较它们的数值范围、零值模式和时间漂移，再看不同量化方案在 state reconstruction 与 update error 上的差异。结论不是“AL8 到处都最好”，而是更细的几条规律：对 dense AdamW，AL8 与所比较的 8-bit AdamW baseline 基本竞争；对 CAME，confidence states 对精度更敏感，低比特会更明显地伤害更新保真，因此更适合用高于二阶矩的存储精度；对 Adafactor，保护敏感张量、并让量化粒度与行/列因子拓扑对齐，会明显优于粗糙的统一分块；对 APOLLO，这类投影空间状态的误差传播路径又不同，说明不能把 projected states 当成普通 dense array 处理。端到端实验覆盖 20K step 的 TinyLlama-1.1B 预训练和 100K step 的 GPT-2 预训练，用来验证这些诊断是否真的对应训练行为。给出的总体结论比较克制：AL8 可以在若干路径上达到与现有 8-bit 基线相近的训练表现，但“一个统一低精度配置适用于所有结构化状态”这个想法并不成立。摘要和正文片段里没有出现完整的最终损失表格数字，因此更该关注趋势和失败案例，而不是只盯一个平均指标。","reading_guide":"如果是做训练系统或优化器的人，建议先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，把四类状态拓扑差异吃透；这决定了后面为什么不能只比较 state methods are designed around dense Adam-style first and second moments, while modern memory-efficient optimizers change both the topology of the stored state and the way that state enters the update. In Adafactor, the second moment is factored into row and column statistics; in CAME, additional confidence states modulate momentum; in APOLLO, moments live in a projected gradient space. The same reconstruction error can therefore induce very different update errors across optimizers. The paper’s core problem is how to quantize these heterogeneous states in a way that respects their numerical distribution, structural layout, and update semantics, and how to evaluate such quantization using update fidelity and real pretraining behavior rather than state MSE alone.","importance":"The main contribution for pretraining research is conceptual as much as practical. It argues that optimizer-state quantization should no longer be treated as a generic '8-bit Adam' trick. As large-scale training increasingly relies on structured-state optimizers such as Adafactor, CAME, and APOLLO, memory reduction depends not only on how many bytes are stored but also on how quantization noise propagates through the optimizer’s update rule. That is directly relevant to training recipes under memory pressure, low-cost continual pretraining, and large-batch scaling where optimizer state is a major systems bottleneck. The paper does not introduce a new training paradigm, but it sharpens the design space: representation, topology, and update semantics must be considered jointly.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"$12","reading_guide":"$13","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读实验表和各 optimizer 的精度分配即可；1.1B/20K 步证据偏小，但 AL8 二阶矩值得内部复现。","en":"Read the tables on per-optimizer precision choices; the 1.1B/20K-step evidence is small, but AL8 second moments are worth an internal replication."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yan Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["optimizer-state","quantization","adamw","pretraining-efficiency","memory-optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22322","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22322","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22316","title":"Does a Modern-Handwriting Warm-Up Help Historical Arabic OCR? search、采样筛选都会改变被平均的样本分布，尤其会把权重推向高分尾部。论文给出一个清晰结论：评估误差不只有采样噪声，还有结构性盲区。增加样本量只能降低噪声，不能补齐没有被测试覆盖的部署方向。这个观点对验证 inference-time scaling、reward model 选择、best-of-N 生成质量和安全性都比较有用。","technical_approach":"方法把验证写成一个线性逆问题。未知可靠性表面为 f，验证干预 v_j 只能观测积分 <v_j, f>，部署目标 k 需要估计 <k, f>。核心定理是：对所有有界可靠性函数 0≤f≤1，部署目标可由验证证据精确识别，当且仅当 k 位于验证核 span{v_1,...,v_p} 中。否则，最大不可辨识宽度等于 k 到该张成空间的 L1 距离，也就是 blind residual。这个量不是上界，而是可达到的最坏情况。论文进一步讨论选择后评估：如果部署目标是在看到评估结果后选出来的，点态置信区间不够，必须对整个目标族同时覆盖。对 iid best-of-N，候选的分数百分位 U 服从均匀分布，best-of-N 选择最大百分位，其部署核为 k_N(u)=N u^{N-1}。如果只审计 n=1,...,m 的 best-of-n，已验证空间对应 degree m-1 的多项式；要认证更大的 N，本质上是在用低阶矩约束推断更尾部的阶统计量目标。论文用有界 Hausdorff moment problem 和 Chebyshev 第二类多项式给出精确前沿。","experiments":"这是一篇以定理为主的工作，不是实验论文。关键结果包括：1）一般核空间下，目标识别当且仅当 k deployment after best-of-N search, reranking, rejection sampling, or other selection mechanisms induces a different target distribution. If that target direction is not represented by the audited directions, repeating the same tests cannot yield an exact certificate.","importance":"The main relevance to LLM work is evaluation and the pretrain–posttrain interface. Verifier-guided decoding, RL selection, best-of-N sampling, and inference-time search all change the population over which reliability is averaged. The paper separates sampling uncertainty from structural blindness. More repetitions reduce noise, but they do not cover a deployment direction that was never audited. This is a useful lens for validating reward models, search-amplified generation, and safety claims under post-hoc model or output selection.","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"This is a theorem-driven paper rather than an empirical study. The key results are: exact identifiability iff the deployment kernel is in the audited span; for normalized kernels and evidence equal to 1/2, the identified interval has exact width equal to the blind residual and is centered at 1/2; post-selection validity for arbitrary measurable selectors requires family-wise simultaneous coverage; and for iid best-of-N, auditing n≤m only constrains low-order polynomial moments, leaving a closed-form worst-case ambiguity for deployment at N>m. The useful connection is test-time scaling for agents. ParallelWorld shows that extra inference-time compute can be spent not only on answer sampling or self-consistency, but also on simulating multiple future world trajectories and using a verifier to select informative branches. This is relevant to search-based reasoning, process verification, and compute allocation at inference time.","technical_approach":"ParallelWorld replaces autoregressive single-trajectory exploration with a tree of simulated future worlds. Given a question, an initial simulator state, and an initial observation, it maintains a set of retained trajectories. Each branch stores a restorable runtime state, including the simulator state, task-specific interaction state, camera pose, and relevant object states. At each step, every retained branch is expanded with executable actions. The action space includes camera movements and rotations, as well as task-dependent physical operations such as picking, placing, pouring, and stacking. The simulator transitions to prospective states and renders their observations. A verifier agent scores the frontier and prunes branches according to a branch-width schedule, keeping multiple hypotheses early and narrowing them later. An answer agent reasons over the highest-ranked root-to-leaf route and stops when confidence is sufficient or the exploration budget is exhausted.","experiments":"The paper reports improvements on embodied understanding benchmarks over sequential active exploration and myopic test-time scaling baselines. 很重要。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode of few-step flow-based language models: generation quality collapses when the number of sampling steps is small, and the authors argue that a major cause is a structural train–inference mismatch in previous-prediction self-conditioning. During training, the self-conditioning input is produced from the current noisy state with no solver step in between. During sampling, the previous prediction has already been folded into the latent by the solver and is then fed again as the explicit self-conditioning input. This creates a coupling between the latent pathway and the self-conditioning pathway that never appears during training. The mismatch becomes worse as step width grows, which is exactly the few-step regime.","importance":"This is an important paper for non-AR language modeling, especially flow-matching and diffusion-style text generation. Its value is not just a practical sampler tweak. It identifies a concrete mechanism behind few-step degradation and shows that self-conditioning is not uniformly beneficial: under strong solver-induced coupling, it can become harmful. That observation should matter beyond the specific models tested here, because many parallel generative samplers rely on some form of self-conditioning or recurrent prediction reuse. The paper is also notable for how much it gains without retraining. Large improvements at fixed NFE suggest that part of the current few-step bottleneck in language flows comes from the sampling recurrence itself, not only from model capacity or training objective quality. For foundation-model researchers, the paper is worth close reading because it turns a vague train–inference mismatch story into a geometric redundancy analysis plus a numerical-integration correction.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"$18","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看方法和少步 ablation；它把 flow LM 的质量问题定位到 solver/self-conditioning 耦合，而不是泛泛调采样器。","en":"Read the method and few-step ablations now; it pins flow-LM degradation on solver/self-conditioning coupling rather than generic sampler tuning."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Bocheng Li","Linli Xu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["flow-matching-lm","self-conditioning","non-autoregressive-generation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22244","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22244","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22090","title":"Semantic Reasoning Denoising: Correcting Language Model Reasoning with Semantic Operators","abstract":"Large language models can produce fluent reasoning traces whose local semantic errors propagate to an incorrect conclusion, while unconstrained self-correction may preserve, amplify, or introduce errors. trajecto","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22090.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"SRD v)，包含错误类型、发生位置、原命题和被污染后的命题。算子库覆盖前提插入/删除、关系反转、中间值替换、步骤重排、结论替换等常见推理错误。前向过程不是高斯加噪，也不是 token-level corruption，而是按顺序组合这些算子，得到一系列语义上连贯但逐渐变脏的推理状态。反向过程训练模型预测当前状态中活跃的语义噪声，并给出对应的局部逆算子，将轨迹往低噪声状态推进。训练数据由三类样本组成：真实错误轨迹、合成加噪轨迹，以及错误轨迹与正确轨迹之间的插值轨迹。推理时进行多轮 denoising，每轮预测一个 inverse operator，并检查其可执行性，避免普通 self-correction 中常见的整段重写、引入新错误或保留旧错误的问题。","experiments":"实验覆盖 13 个任务，包括数学推理、代码生成和通用理解。作者报告只需要几千个 transition pairs 就能训练 SRD adapter。在 6 个 in-domain benchmark 上，SRD 平均分为 67.5，比同 backbone 中最强 baseline 高 3.2 分，并且在 6 个任务上全部领先；其中 5 个任务是同 backbone 方法中的最好结果。迁移实验显示，SRD 在 Llama backbone 上保持竞争力，在 Qwen3 transfer 设置下取得最高平均分。关键实验信息在于样本效率和跨数据迁移，而不是模型规模扩展；现有结果还不能说明这种 operatorized denoising 能直接替代预训练阶段的语言建模目标。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。重点看 Method 中 operator 定义、operator distance、forward/reverse transition 的形式化部分，以及 Figure 1、Figure 2。实验部分优先看 in-domain 六任务均值、same-backbone 对比和 transfer 结果。值得深挖的问题有两个：第一，错误算子库的覆盖率和人工设计成本；第二，局部逆转移是否能扩展到开放域长链推理，因为真实推理错误往往不是单一可定位 proposition 的替换。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"SRD studies how to correct local semantic errors in reasoning traces through constrained iterative edits, instead of regenerating an entire chain of thought with an unconstrained self-correction prompt.","importance":"The work is not a core pretraining paper. It is closer to reasoning refinement and small-adapter training. Its value for pretraining research lies in the modeling abstraction: noise is represented as an executable semantic error operator, not as token masking, token replacement, or a standard discrete diffusion transition. This gives a useful process-level analogue for diffusion language modeling. The key idea is to define noise magnitude by operator distance over reasoning states, then learn local inverse transitions. That is a more structured alternative to surface-form denoising.","technical_approach":"A correct reasoning trajectory r0 is treated as the clean state. Corrupted trajectories are produced by composing semantic error operators. Each operator z=(κ, ℓ, u, v) records the error type, the step location, the original proposition, and the corrupted proposition. The operator library includes premise insertion and deletion, relation reversal, intermediate-value substitution, step reordering, and conclusion substitution. The forward process applies these operators to create coherent but progressively noisier reasoning states. The reverse process trains the model to identify the active semantic noise and apply the corresponding local repair. Training uses real error trajectories, synthetic noised trajectories, and interpolated trajectories between erroneous and correct reasoning. At inference time, SRD runs several denoising rounds. Each round predicts one inverse operator and checks whether it is applicable, which makes the edit more constrained than standard self-correction.","experiments":"The paper evaluates on 13 tasks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and general understanding. The authors report that a few thousand transition pairs are enough to train the SRD adapter. On six in-domain benchmarks, SRD reaches an average score of 67.5, improving over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 3.2 points and leading it on all six tasks. It is also the best same-backbone method on five of them. Transfer experiments show competitive results on Llama and the best reported Qwen3 transfer average. The evidence mainly supports sample-efficient reasoning repair; it does not yet establish this objective as a scalable replacement for pretraining-time language modeling.","reading_guide":"A 20-minute skim is enough. Focus on the method sections defining the operator tuple, operator distance, and forward/reverse transitions, plus Figures 1 and 2. In the experiments, inspect the six in-domain benchmark average, same-backbone comparisons, and transfer results. The most useful questions to keep in mind are whether the hand-designed operator library covers real reasoning failures, and whether local inverse edits remain reliable for open-ended long-horizon reasoning.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"想法值得看，尤其是 operator 噪声定义；若标注靠弱规则或自生成，实验结论只能当方向信号。","en":"The operator-noise formulation is worth reading; if the labels come from weak rules or self-generation, treat the results as directional only."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yujiao Yang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-language-modeling","reasoning","semantic-denoising","self-correction","latent-reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22090","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22090","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22274","title":"Length-Adaptive Decoding for Masked Diffusion Machine Translation","abstract":"Machine translation tests masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) because every source token must be rendered faithfully, while fixed canvas decoding must choose target length before denoising. Existing masked diffusion decoding work mainly studies token unmasking order, leaving this length decision under-explored despite its direct effect on coverage and redundancy. We introduce Entropy-Valley (EV), a training-free length selector that scores candidate target canvases by mean predictive entrop","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22274.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 前向的熵信号选择长度，说明 dLLM 内部已经暴露出可用于规划生成边界的不确定性信号。这个思路可迁移到摘要、代码补全、结构化生成等固定 canvas 场景。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Entropy-Valley。给定源句后，先根据语言方向构造一组候选目标长度，候选来自源长乘以若干源到目标长度比例，并额外保留 EOS 位置。对每个候选 canvas，模型只做一次 all-mask forward，不进行完整去噪；然后计算每个目标槽位预测分布的平均熵。熵最低的长度被选为最终 canvas，再用原来的 minimum-entropy decoding schedule 解码。这里的关键选择是用模型自身的“准备好填这个长度”的程度，而不是预测参考译文长度。过短 canvas 会让多个源内容被压进少数槽位，分布更不确定；过长 canvas 会产生没有明确 token 归属的空余位置，也会提高熵。与非自回归 MT 的 fertility/length predictor 不同，EV 不需要额外训练目标；与 corpus-level ratio 不同，它按句子自适应；与 reveal-order 方法不同，它改变的是去噪前的空间大小，而不是去噪顺序。","experiments":"实验设置是 LLaDA-8B 的 LoRA-SFT masked 为什么能比较候选长度。实验部分重点看 reference length oracle、fixed ratio、EV 的 COMET-22 对比，以及 placeholder/number retention 的错误分析。若关注 diffusion LM 解码机制，可以继续看 reveal-order 控制实验：它能帮助区分“选多长”和“按什么顺序揭示”这两个问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets target-length selection in masked diffusion machine translation. Unlike an autoregressive decoder, a masked diffusion decoder must receive a target canvas before denoising starts. If the canvas is too short, content is dropped; if it is too long, the model can repeat, hallucinate, or dilute the translation. The key point is that canvas length is a separate bottleneck from token reveal order.","importance":"The work is useful for studying diffusion language models because it exposes a structural inference-time issue in fixed-canvas generation. The contribution is not a new MT architecture, but a clean way to ask whether a pretrained masked diffusion backbone can choose its own generation space without a trained length predictor. The result suggests that all-mask uncertainty is a meaningful planning signal. This idea may matter for other conditional generation settings where diffusion LMs must decide output extent before iterative denoising.","technical_approach":"Entropy-Valley builds a small candidate set of target canvas lengths from source-length ratios for each translation direction, with one extra slot reserved for EOS. For each candidate length, it runs a single all-mask forward pass and computes the mean predictive entropy over target positions. The length with the lowest mean entropy is selected, and standard minimum-entropy decoding is then run on that canvas. The method keeps the backbone, training loss, and unmasking schedule unchanged. Its distinction from classic non-autoregressive MT length prediction is that it introduces no fertility model or auxiliary objective. Its distinction from fixed corpus ratios is that the decision is sentence-specific. Its distinction from reveal-order work is that it chooses the canvas before denoising rather than changing which masks are revealed first.","experiments":"The main setting is a LoRA-SFT LLaDA-8B masked diffusion MT system on WMT22 En→Zh, Zh→En, and En→De. Entropy-Valley recovers 64.9%, 65.3%, and 33.0% of the COMET-22 gain obtained by using reference target lengths in the three directions. On WMT22 En→Zh, placeholder retention improves from 66.9% to 89.1% over the fixed-ratio baseline, and number retention improves from 77.6% to 81.3%. The evaluation also includes statistical tests, bidirectional expert human evaluation, coverage and error-type analysis, reveal-order controls, a matched-data LLaMA-3-8B autoregressive baseline, and cross-backbone checks on Dream-Base and DiffuLLaMA.","reading_guide":"A 30–40 minute skim is enough for most readers. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22898.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"扩散语言模型（DLM）推理快但质量弱。论文聚焦一个很具体的问题：为什么把常规知识蒸馏（logit-level teacher 分布过尖（spiky）与 DLM 在高 mask ratio 下分布更平（flat）的 mismatch 讲清楚，并给出一个可落地的替代：利用 DLM 自身噪声/掩码强度构造不同难度视角做自蒸馏。对后续做 diffusion/any-order LM 的训练稳定性与质量提升，属于“可复用的训练技巧”级别贡献。","technical_approach":"SelFusion 的核心是把同一个 DLM 变成两个“模式”来互相蒸馏：\n1) easy mode vs hard mode：对同一训练样本做两次 forward，但掩码率不同。hard mode 用原始较高 masking；easy mode 用更低 masking，让模型看到更多上下文，通常能给出更可靠的 token 分布。\n2) 关键问题：easy mode 不总是更对，而且低 masking 会让分布更尖、更自信；这种“错得很自信”会把 hard mode 带偏。\n3) bidirectional KD（逐 token 决策蒸馏方向）：不是固定 easy→hard，而是根据 token-level correctness/置信度在两者间动态选 teacher。\n- 两者都对：选对当前预测 token 给更高概率的一方作为 teacher。\n- 两者都错：选对 ground-truth token 给更高概率的一方作为 teacher（避免跟着错误 top-1 走）。\n- 一对一错：用正确的一方当 teacher。\n4) 论文动机里强调“student-friendly teacher”：easy mode 的分布形态更接近 hard mode（同架构同参数），比 AR teacher 的极尖分布更可蒸馏；同时又通过逐 token 方向选择抑制 easy mode 的过度自信副作用。\n整体上，这是把 DLM 的 noising/masking 机制当作“内生的课程学习 + 自蒸馏信号生成器”，而不是额外引入一个外部强 teacher。","experiments":"全文给出的关键信息点主要是现象与机制证据：\n- AR→DLM logit KD 的 mismatch：AR teacher 往往 top-1 概率接近 100%，而 DLM 在约 50% masking 时 top-1 概率约 60% 左右，导致分布匹配困难。\n- easy mode 相对 hard mode：训练过程中 easy mode 对正确 token 的概率平均高约 10%（论文用来支撑“更适合作 teacher”）。\n- easy mode 的风险：低 masking 会让分布更 peaked，且在错误 token masked / diffusion 目标上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Diffusion language models decode in parallel but lag behind AR LLMs in generation quality. The paper targets a practical training question: why conventional knowledge distillation (logit-level or sequence-level) often yields marginal gains or even hurts DLMs, and how to make distillation effective when (1) AR→DLM distributions are mismatched and (2) strong DLM teachers are unavailable.","importance":"The main value is a reusable recipe for non-AR language modeling: distillation can be internal (no external teacher) and can be bidirectional rather than a fixed teacher→student pipeline. The paper also provides a concrete diagnosis of AR-to-DLM logit mismatch (overly peaky AR distributions vs flatter DLM distributions under masking), which is useful when designing training objectives for diffusion/any-order LMs.","technical_approach":"SelFusion turns a single DLM into two “views” via different masking ratios and performs self-distillation between them.\n- Two-mode forward: an easy mode with lower masking (more context) and a hard mode with higher masking (standard DLM setting).\n- Naive easy→hard distillation is unsafe because low masking produces sharper, overconfident distributions, including on wrong tokens.\n- Bidirectional, token-wise distillation: for each token, choose the distillation direction based on correctness and confidence.\n  * both correct: distill from the mode assigning higher probability to the predicted token.\n  * both wrong: distill from the mode assigning higher probability to the ground-truth token.\n  * one correct: distill from the correct mode.\nThis design keeps the “teacher” distribution close to the student (same model family) while avoiding the failure mode where the easy view is confidently wrong.","experiments":"Key evidence and reported outcomes include:\n- Distribution mismatch: AR teachers often put ~100% mass on top-1, while DLMs at ~50% masking show ~60% top-1 probability, making logit matching ineffective.\n- Easy vs hard: the easy mode assigns ~10% higher probability to the correct token on average during training.\n- Failure mode prevalence: cases where both modes are wrong occur in ~40% of tokens, motivating the special handling in the bidirectional rule.\n- On multiple instruction-following benchmarks, SelFusion consistently beats standard KD baselines that rely on external teachers, and is reported to even surpass the teacher models (both DLM and LLM teachers). This makes generation costly, especially when the model continues to recompute tokens that are already stable. To address these limitations, we propose CAI-DLLM, a training-free inference method that uses first-step confidence to guide denoising and reduce inference time. Specifically, CAI-DLLM commits easy tokens earlier, allocates more denoising steps to harder toke","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22646.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 diffusion language model 推理时的重复计算问题：masked diffusion LM 能并行更新多个 token，但固定步数 denoising 会让已经稳定的 token 继续参与完整前向计算，导致生成成本偏高。","importance":"这是一篇 diffusion LM 推理优化论文，对预训练研究的直接价值在于揭示了离散扩散语言模型的收敛异质性：不同位置、不同 block、不同置信度 token 的稳定速度差异很大。这个现象不仅能用于 decoding，也提示训练目标和噪声调度可以更显式地利用 token difficulty。方法本身是 training-free，对已有 LLaDA、Dream 这类 masked diffusion LM 有工程参考价值。","technical_approach":"CAI-dLLM 使用第一步 denoising 的置信度作为 token difficulty 信号。这个信号在标准解码中本来就会计算，因此不需要额外模型、额外前向或权重更新。方法将 token 按 first-step confidence 分成不同难度，容易的 token 早提交，困难 token 分配更多 denoising budget。论文使用的 token budget 包括 8、32、64 步。它还引入 block-level adaptive threshold schedule：后续输出 block 因为可见上下文更多，收敛通常更快，所以 block end threshold 随 block 递减。最后加入 low-yield grinding phase detector，如果连续 K 步新提交 token 很少，就提前停止后期低收益 denoising。与只做 KV cache 或 early layer skipping 的方法不同，CAI-dLLM 不是降低每一步成本，而是改变每个 token 和每个 block 的计算分配。","experiments":"论文在 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 和 Dream-7B-Instruct 上评估，任务覆盖数学、代码、推理、常识和长上下文。动机实验使用 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 在 GSM8K 上生成 256 token，并划分为 4 个 64-token block，统计 1,319 个测试 prompt。结果显示 first-step confidence 对稳定时间有较强预测性：c_i(0) > 0.50 的 token 通常在 5 步内稳定；c_i(0) < 0.25 的 token 往往在 30 步后仍会变化。论文报告 CAI-dLLM 能减少推理时间，同时保持任务质量；具体速度收益和质量损失需要查看实验表格，提供内容中没有完整可核验数字。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。重点看动机部分的 Figure 1：它展示 hidden-state drift、block 收敛差异和 first-step confidence 与稳定时间的关系。方法部分重点看 token budget 分配、block threshold schedule 和 low-yield early exit。实验部分建议关注 speed-quality tradeoff，而不是只看平均分。若研究 diffusion LM 训练，可以进一步思考 first-step confidence 是否能作为训练阶段的 curriculum、loss reweighting 或 adaptive noise schedule 信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets redundant computation in diffusion language model inference. Masked diffusion LMs can update many positions in parallel, but fixed-step denoising keeps recomputing tokens that have already stabilized.","importance":"The main value for pretraining researchers is the empirical observation that discrete diffusion LMs have strong convergence heterogeneity across tokens and output blocks. Easy tokens stabilize quickly, while low-confidence tokens need many more refinement steps. This matters beyond decoding: it suggests that token difficulty, confidence calibration, and adaptive noise schedules may be useful signals when designing or training diffusion LMs.","technical_approach":"CAI-dLLM is a training-free inference controller. It uses the confidence from the first denoising step as a token-level difficulty estimate, which comes for free because the first forward pass is already required. High-confidence tokens are committed early, low-confidence tokens receive larger denoising budgets, and budgets are chosen from 8, 32, and 64 steps. The method also uses a block-level adaptive threshold schedule: later output blocks receive lower end thresholds because they tend to converge faster with more committed context. A low-yield detector exits late denoising when too few new tokens are committed for K consecutive steps. Compared with KV-cache-based or layer-skipping methods, CAI-dLLM changes the allocation of denoising steps across tokens and blocks rather than only reducing the cost of each step.","experiments":"The evaluation uses LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-7B-Instruct on math, code, reasoning, commonsense, and long-context tasks. The motivation study runs LLaDA-8B-Instruct on GSM8K with 256 generated tokens split into four 64-token blocks over 1,319 prompts. injection","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22354.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Delta-Rule 模型统一写成仿射递推形式，即状态由上一时刻状态经过转移矩阵更新，再叠加当前 token 的注入项。RWKV-7 的固定状态在长序列中会受到两类力量影响：一类是持续 decay，使弱更新位置维持在低幅值；另一类是不均匀注入和转移放大，使少数通道累积异常大值。SANE 的处理很直接：只在 chunk 边界对终止状态做逐元素压缩，形式为 \\(\\widetilde{S}=\\tau\\tanh(S/\\tau)\\)。当 \\(|S|\\ll\\tau\\) 时近似恒等映射，保留低幅值背景；当 \\(|S|\\) 接近或超过阈值时逐渐压缩，阻止异常值跨 chunk 继续传播。这样做避免了在 token 级递推内部插入非线性，因此不破坏 affine recurrence 和 associative scan 的并行计算结构。阈值由输入相关尺度和系数 \\(\\alpha\\) 控制，实验中安全区间主要落在 \\(\\alpha=3\\) 到 \\(5\\)。额外参数量低于 0.1%。","experiments":"实验以 0.4B RWKV-7 为 proof of concept，在已有 checkpoint 上通过 SFT 引入 SANE。短上下文数学推理上，\\(\\alpha\\ge training context. The paper asks how to suppress extreme-context state explosions without changing the parallel chunk computation inside the recurrence.","importance":"The useful contribution is the failure analysis. The paper shows that RWKV-7 does not simply suffer from global state saturation under 100M-token extrapolation. Instead, most state entries remain small while a small number of localized entries grow into large hotspots. This gives long-context recurrent LM research a concrete stability target: not just memory complexity, but the distribution, dynamic range, and cross-chunk propagation of the recurrent state.","technical_approach":"The authors formulate Delta-Rule models as affine state updates, where the previous state is transformed and then receives a token-dependent injection. Their diagnosis is that persistent decay keeps most weakly updated entries small, while uneven injections and transition-induced amplification let a few directions accumulate extreme values. SANE applies an element-wise boundary transform, \\(\\widetilde{S}=\\tau\\tanh(S/\\tau)\\), to the terminal state of each chunk. Small values are nearly unchanged; values near or above the adaptive threshold are compressed. Because the transform is applied only between chunks, the intra-chunk affine recurrence and associative-scan parallelism are preserved. The method adds less than 0.1% parameters, with the compression scale controlled by \\(\\alpha\\).","experiments":"The proof-of-concept uses a 0.4B RWKV-7 checkpoint and introduces SANE through supervised fine-tuning. On 11 short-context math reasoning benchmarks, settings with \\(\\alpha\\ge 3\\) match the baseline: 43.30–44.23 average accuracy versus 43.88 for the original model, with no statistically significant difference reported. Aggressive compression with \\(\\alpha\\le 2\\) hurts short-context performance. Under extreme-context evaluation, the baseline state norm grows beyond \\(10^{19}\\) near 20M tokens and eventually overflows. SANE remains numerically stable after a 100M-token prefix. Functional accuracy, however, depends strongly on the threshold: \\(\\alpha=5\\) retains 33.46 average accuracy, \\(\\alpha=7\\) drops to 18.45, and \\(\\alpha\\ge 8\\) collapses close to zero despite numerical stability. The key empirical message is that bounded states are not sufficient; the retained state must still carry usable information.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough for most readers. Prior lower bounds for the standard τ-leaping sampler under the uniform forward process scale linearly with the ambient dimension d, which would largely remove the parallel-generation advantage over autoregressive decoding. The central question is whether this dependence is inherent to uniform diffusion or caused by the sampler. The paper also analyzes remasking diffusion, where tokens can be revised during generation but the sampling-efficiency theory is still limited.","importance":"The main relevance is at the interface between non-autoregressive language modeling objectives and samplers. Uniform diffusion, masking diffusion, and remasking diffusion are all plausible alternatives or complements to next-token AR modeling. The paper argues that the poor dimension dependence known for τ-leaping is not a fundamental limitation of uniform-state diffusion. With a better first-order sampler, sampling can adapt to low-dimensional or structured target distributions. This matters for large-scale diffusion LMs because the practical cost is determined by the combination of corruption process, denoiser, and sampler, not by the training objective alone.","technical_approach":"The paper focuses on first-order samplers: each discretization step uses a single model evaluation, while still allowing many coordinates to be updated in parallel. It proposes a sampler based on a leave-one-out denoiser for both uniform and remasking processes. The leave-one-out design avoids treating the current value of the coordinate being predicted as reliable conditioning information, which helps prevent erroneous intermediate tokens from reinforcing themselves. Compared with monotone unmasking in vanilla masking diffusion, the sampler can revise earlier decisions. Compared with one-transition-at-a-time samplers such as uniformization, it preserves the main parallelism advantage of discrete diffusion.","experiments":"The evidence described in the provided full text is primarily theoretical rather than empirical. The key result is that the linear dependence on d for τ-leaping is a sampler artifact, not an intrinsic barrier for the uniform forward process. For both uniform and remasking diffusion, the leave-one-out first-order sampler achieves adaptive guarantees that depend on intrinsic dimensionality or structural complexity of the target distribution rather than simply on ambient dimension. The analysis also highlights why correction of denoising mistakes becomes necessary when many coordinates are updated in the same sampling step.","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is sufficient for most pretraining researchers. Existing suffix dropout methods overlook the structural heterogeneity of different suffix regions and re-initialize suffix tokens at each timestep, discarding evolving information during iterative denoising.","importance":"Introduces a training-free structured suffix modeling method for accelerating DLM inference. question than the usual “attention looks sparse, so KV-cache should be compressible.” It studies when attention is actually compressible, and under which contract. The authors separate four notions that are often conflated: having only a few large weights, needing only a few tokens to retain a target probability mass, needing only a compact state to preserve the attention output, and preserving downstream task performance. The paper’s main point is that these are different objects, and that many existing KV-compression intuitions only address the first one.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is conceptual. First, it reframes KV compression around the quantity being preserved: probability mass, attention output, or task signal. That matters beyond serving, because cache compression is also a statement about what state future queries truly need. Second, the analysis points back to training-time questions. Unlike online serving, which is typically optimized for request-level latency and throughput, a small number of long-tail generations can dominate the end-to-end makespan of an entire rollout step. In practice, rollout requests are often routed uniformly across replicas, which can place extremely long g","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22788.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章解决的是大规模 LLM rollout 中的长尾 makespan 问题。RLVR、on-policy distillation、合成数据生成和 pass@k 评测都需要同步收集一批 rollout；训练步骤必须等最慢的那几个样本结束才能继续。由于输出长度分布高度偏斜，少量超长 generation 会拖住整个 step。现有系统常按均匀路由或一般吞吐优化来分配请求，但这并不适合 rollout 这种以 step 完成时间为目标的场景。论文要回答的是：在不知道真实 completion length 的在线环境里，如何识别并隔离长尾请求，同时动态分配 replica 容量，来降低整个 rollout step 的完工时间。","importance":"对预训练研究员而言，这不是模型算法创新，而是 post-train / synthetic data pipeline 的关键系统问题。它的重要性在于，很多 RL 或 OPD 工作把 rollout 成本当成固定背景，但实际训练 wall-clock 往往被长尾 generation 主导。TailSieve 的价值是把“长尾请求该怎么调度”从经验工程变成了一个有 oracle 结构、可在线逼近的问题。对做 RL pretrain、continual post-train、或大规模采样评测的人，这篇文章提供了一个很实用的视角：同步 rollout 的瓶颈不是平均 token/s，而是 tail isolation 与 replica balancing。它不改变训练目标，也不引入 policy lag，因此比异步方案更容易接到现有训练栈里。","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"从引言能确认的实验重点有三类。第一类是 oracle 分析与近似性验证。作者展示了在已知长度的理想条件下，tail isolation with balancing 的结构优于均匀分配，而简单 top-k 隔离已能恢复大部分 oracle gain。这里最值得看的是 Figure 2，因为它说明为什么“把最长的几个样本单独放”不是拍脑袋，而是接近最优结构。\n\n第二类是 tail signal 的有效性。作者用 partial rollout 截断后仍未完成的 prompt groups 作为下一轮 tail 候选，并在 Section 2.3、Appendix B、Figure 3 中说明这种信号具有较高 recall。这个结果很关键，因为 TailSieve 的在线可行性就建立在“长尾具有短期持续性”上。如果 recall 不够高，后面的 two-pool routing 就站不住。\n\n第三类是端到端系统收益。TailSieve 联合控制 tail workload 和 replica allocation，在 rollout workload 演化时持续调整。引言明确声称它能降低同步 rollout 的 step-level makespan，并且不牺牲 on-policy 性质。给到的全文片段没有完整实验表和具体数字，因此不能编造提升百分比、GPU 数量或模型规模。可以确定的是，实验比较对象至少包括均匀路由、基于长度预测的 skewness-aware dispatch、以及异步/partial-rollout 类方案的设计取舍；评价重点是 step 完成时间，而不是单请求延迟。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按“理论结构—在线信号—控制器实现”三步看。先读 Section 2.1 和 2.2，确认 offline optimum 为什么会呈现 size 和 replica split。\n\n如果只想判断是否值得接入自己的 RL/OPD pipeline，优先看三处：Figure 2、Figure 3、主实验表。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主要思想。若要深挖，建议重点检查两点：第一，prompt group 的定义是否依赖特定任务形态，换到更异质的 rollout workload 是否还保留长度持续性；第二，concurrency-throughput model 的拟合是否稳定，特别是在不同 batch shape、KV cache 压力和多机通信条件下是否需要频繁重估。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the long-tail makespan problem in large-scale LLM rollouts. In RL with verifiable rewards, on-policy distillation, synthetic data generation, and sampling-heavy evaluation, optimization often waits for a synchronized batch of generations to finish. Because output lengths are highly skewed, a few extremely long generations can dominate the completion time of the entire rollout step. Uniform routing across replicas is poorly matched to this objective. The paper asks how to identify and isolate likely tail requests online, and how to allocate replica capacity accordingly, when true completion lengths are unknown before generation.","importance":"For foundation-model researchers, this is a systems paper rather than a modeling paper, but it matters because rollout cost is often the wall-clock bottleneck in modern post-training pipelines. In each layer, fine-grained local features are compressed bottom-up into a small set of proxy states; expensive global interactions are performed only in the compressed proxy space; the globally contextualized p","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_23463.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"超长上下文语言模型和高分辨率生成模型面临注意力计算和 KV Cache 随序列长度呈二次方增长的瓶颈。现有的压缩或近似方法往往在信息保真度和全局交互成本之间面临两难：要么丢失细粒度信息导致长程检索能力下降，要么保留过多信息导致加速效果有限。","importance":"提出了一种通用的双流代理架构 ProxyFormer，通过解耦信息保真度和全局交互成本，在保持局部细节的同时，以极低成本实现全局交互，为超长序列建模提供了一种极具潜力的架构级解决方案。","technical_approach":"ProxyFormer 将输入组织为两个平行的特征流：细粒度/局部流（保持与输入元素一一对应）和代理流（通过压缩局部块得到少量代理向量）。每层执行“自底向上压缩 -> 代理空间交互 -> 自顶向下解压和注入”的闭环。核心创新在于局部流在层间保持残差连接，使得未被某一层压缩吸收的细节可以传递到更深层并在后续压缩中被重新利用；同时，代理流携带的全局信息通过解压反向注入局部流。这种机制允许模型在压缩比为 P 的情况下，将代理空间注意力的序列长度降至 L/P，同时保持局部流的信息完整性。","experiments":"论文在语言模型困惑度、多针 NIAH（Needle-In-A-Haystack）、内存/速度基准测试以及图像生成任务上进行了初步实验。结果表明，ProxyFormer 在显著降低计算和内存开销的同时，保持了与标准仅解码器基线相当甚至更好的性能，特别是在长上下文检索任务中表现出色。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 ProxyFormer Methodology 部分，理解双流架构的设计理念和层间信息流转机制。特别关注 Algorithm 1 和 Figure 1，它们清晰地展示了前向传播过程。实验部分可关注多针 NIAH 测试结果，这是验证长上下文信息保留能力的关键指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Ultra-long-context language models and high-resolution generative models face a bottleneck where attention computation and KV cache grow quadratically with sequence length. Existing compression or approximation methods often struggle with a trade-off between information fidelity and global interaction cost: either losing fine-grained information (degrading long-range retrieval) or retaining too much (limiting acceleration).","importance":"Proposes ProxyFormer, a general dual-stream proxy architecture that decouples information fidelity and global interaction cost. It achieves global interaction at extremely low cost while preserving local details, offering a highly promising architectural solution for ultra-long sequence modeling.","technical_approach":"ProxyFormer organizes input into two parallel feature streams: a fine/local stream (maintaining one-to-one correspondence with input elements) and a proxy stream (a small number of proxy vectors obtained by compressing local blocks). Each layer executes a closed loop: 'bottom-up compression -> proxy space interaction -> top-down decompression and injection'. The core innovation is that the local stream maintains residual connections across layers, allowing details not absorbed by one layer's compression to pass to deeper layers for reuse; meanwhile, global information from the proxy stream is injected back into the local stream via decompression. This mechanism allows the model, at a compression ratio P, to reduce the sequence length for proxy space attention to L/P while preserving the local stream's information integrity.","experiments":"Preliminary experiments were conducted on language model perplexity, multi-needle NIAH, memory/speed benchmarks, and image generation tasks. Results show that ProxyFormer significantly reduces computational and memory overhead while maintaining performance comparable to or better than standard decoder-only baselines, particularly excelling in long-context retrieval tasks.","reading_guide":"Focus on the ProxyFormer Methodology section to understand the design philosophy of the dual-stream architecture and the inter-layer information flow mechanism. We show this is fragile on long-form audio: prefill attention concentrates near the audio start (an attention-sink effect), while decode-time attention distributes broadly, and the two rankings overlap weakly. We propose WnW (Waxing-and-Waning KV cache), which classifies KV-heads","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22704.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.SD"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长语音输入下 speech LLM 的 KV cache 管理问题。作者指出，现有很多 KV 压缩方法都默认“prefill 阶段测到的重要位置，解码阶段也会一直重要”，于是一次性打分、一次性保留、被丢掉的位置永不恢复。这个假设在长文本上已经不总成立，在长语音上更脆弱：prefill 注意力容易被音频开头吸住，出现明显的 attention sink，而真正生成文本时，模型对整段音频的关注更分散。于是 prefill-only 压缩会在紧预算下把后面真正需要的音频位置过早丢掉，导致生成质量大幅下降，甚至无法正常结束生成。","importance":"这不是 pretrain 论文，但对做统一多模态 LM、长上下文建模和 serving 的人有直接参考价值。第一，它给了一个很具体的反例：不能把 text LLM 上成立得还算勉强的 KV 压缩假设，直接搬到 speech LLM。模态不同，attention dynamics 也不同。第二，它把“可恢复性”提成核心设计原则：压缩不是只看保留率，而要看被丢弃的信息是否还有回来的路径。第三，方法虽然服务于推理，但背后反映的是模型在 prefill 与 decode 两个阶段对音频信息的使用方式不同，这对未来做音频 tokenization、长语音预训练目标、甚至 streaming/online 建模都有启发。","technical_approach":"WnW 的设计分成离线 head triage 和在线 chunk recall 两部分。离线阶段，作者对每个 KV head 计算两个指标：voice score 和 head sensitivity。前者衡量这个 head 的注意力有多少真正落在音频上，可以理解为它的 audio-grounding 程度；后者衡量压缩这个 head 的 KV 后，对模型损失或输出质量有多敏感，文中用梯度相关的敏感度估计。基于这两个量，所有 KV heads 被分成三类。anchor heads 全量保留音频 KV 在 GPU 上，不参与压缩，作用是做 decode-time importance observer；tidal heads 只在 GPU 上保留一部分，其余补集放在 CPU，需要时按 chunk 召回；fixed heads 则采用更传统的静态保留，只留 prefill 选出的子集在 GPU，不做召回。在线阶段，解码每一步都读取 anchor heads 对音频位置的注意力，把 token-level 分数聚合成 chunk-level importance，再把最相关的 chunk 从 CPU 召回到 tidal heads 的 GPU cache，同时把已经不重要的 chunk 换出。也就是说，GPU 上的音频 KV 不是单调减少，而是随生成过程 waxing-and-waning。这个设计和文本 KV 管理的差别在于：它不是用 query-summary 相似度之类的通用启发式，而是直接利用少量保留完整上下文的 anchor heads 作为“观察窗”，让 recall 信号与真实语音时间轴对齐。","experiments":"实验最关键的发现有两层。第一层是现象验证：在 LibriSpeech-Long 上，用 Voxtral 分析 prefill 与 decode 的注意力分布，prefill 的质量明显集中在音频开头，而整个 decode 过程累计的注意力分布更平，二者 top-K 位置的 Jaccard overlap 很低。这直接说明 prefill-only 排名不能可靠预测 decode 需求。第二层是方法效果：在两个 3B 骨干上，Voxtral-mini-3b 和 Qwen2.5-Omni-3B，WnW 在只保留 20% 音频 token 于 GPU 的情况下，WER 仍只比 full cache 高约 1.6 个点；而 prefill-only baselines 在这个预算下会明显失真，甚至出现无法终止生成、WER 超过 100% 的情况。作者还报告了跨语言、跨任务、跨领域的泛化，以及在 24B backbone 上相对其他 KV 管理基线仍有优势。另一个值得注意的点是 recall 开销：论文强调 CPU–GPU chunk recall 的额外代价有限，说明这个方案不是靠牺牲过多吞吐换来的。方法分析里还比较了不同 head role 分配、不同 chunk 策略、以及 anchor signal 的有效性，支撑其三类 head 划分不是拍脑袋设定。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Figure 1 和对应分析，确认 prefill/decode attention mismatch 这个现象是否站得住；这是整篇论文的前提。然后看方法部分的 head triage：voice score、head sensitivity 各自怎么定义，为什么要同时用两个指标，而不是只按注意力大小分头。接着看在线 recall 机制，重点是 chunk-level 聚合、召回/换出策略，以及 anchor heads 为什么能作为可靠 observer。实验部分优先看 20% GPU budget 这类紧预算结果，因为方法优势在宽松预算下不会那么明显；再看 failure-to-terminate 的案例，这能说明 prefill-only 方法的问题不是小幅退化，而是结构性失效。预计 25 分钟能读完主线；如果你做多模态 serving 或长语音模型，建议把附录里的实现细节和 overhead 分析也看掉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies KV-cache compression for long-form speech LLMs. Its starting point is that most existing methods make a strong assumption: positions that look important during prefill will remain the important ones during decoding, so the cache can be pruned once and never revisited. The authors show that this assumption breaks badly for long audio. Prefill attention is heavily biased toward the beginning of the audio sequence, exhibiting an attention-sink pattern, while decode-time attention is spread much more broadly over the clip. As a result, prefill-only compression can discard audio positions that later become necessary, causing severe quality degradation and even generation failures under tight GPU budgets.","importance":"This is an inference paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it matters for researchers working on multimodal foundation models, long-context systems, and serving. First, it provides a concrete modality-specific warning: KV-cache heuristics that are acceptable for text do not transfer cleanly to speech. Second, it elevates recoverability to a central design principle. Compression is not only about what stays on GPU, but also about whether evicted information can come back when the model’s needs change during decoding. Third, the work exposes a deeper modeling fact: speech LLMs use audio context differently at prefill and decode time. That observation is relevant beyond serving, including future work on audio tokenization, long-form speech objectives, and streaming multimodal modeling.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"Start with Figure 1 and the accompanying analysis of prefill-versus-decode attention. If that mismatch is not convincing, the rest of the method is less compelling. Then read the head-triage section carefully: understand how voice score and head sensitivity are defined, and why both are needed instead of a single attention-based ranking. After that, focus on the online recall mechanism, especially chunk-level aggregation, swap policy, and the role of anchor heads as reliable observers. In the experiments, prioritize the tight-budget settings such as 20% on-GPU audio retention, because that is where the method’s advantage becomes structurally visible. Also look at the failure-to-terminate cases; they show that prefill-only compression is not merely slightly worse but can break decoding altogether. st","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21952.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何对 Mamba-2 的 Structured State Space Duality（SSD）层做后训练量化（PTQ），在保持接近 FP16 精度的同时，尽可能让推理路径“持续 INT8”（persistent INT8 path），从而真正降低 SSD 在实际系统里的 DRAM 访问、延迟和吞吐瓶颈；并解决直接套用 Transformer 量化方法在 SSD SSD 特别敏感”拆成可操作的统计与算子层原因。对预训练研究的间接影响在于：如果 SSM/SSD 作为基座路线成立，推理成本与部署可行性会反过来影响架构选择与训练配方（例如是否值得为 SSD 训练更大模型、是否能在边缘端跑）。","technical_approach":"方法由两条主线组成：\n1) 让 INT8 路径不断：作者观察 SSD 中存在“通道变化/形状变化的 activation”，且这些 activation 会跨模块被反复调用；如果量化点放错，会频繁 dequant/quant 或回到 FP16，导致 DRAM 往返和延迟上升。SSDi8 的做法是在更合适的节点对这些 activation 做 INT8 量化并缓存复用，尽量保证从输入到输出的执行链路保持 INT8。\n2) 处理 element-wise 打断 INT8 的问题：SSD 里 element-wise 乘法与矩阵乘法交织，常见实现会迫使中间结果回到高精度或引入额外转换。论文提出一种“sparse-aware reformulation”，把 element-wise 与 GEMM 的组织方式重写，使得关键路径仍可用 INT8 kernel 执行，并给出数学层面的等价性/正确性保证（至少在论文叙述中强调了 formal guarantee）。\n3) 针对 SSD 的统计结构做精度修复：作者指出 SSD 把 model dimension 拆成 head 数与 per-head 维度后，两条轴的分布/离群点（outlier）特性不同；若按传统“整维度统一量化尺度”会放大量化误差。SSDi8 显式利用这种二维结构做更细的量化误差控制。\n4) 误差均值校正：引入基于 per-channel error mean 的 correction term，用来抵消系统性偏差，提升 PTQ 后的稳定性与精度。\n整体上它不是提出新的量化理论，而是把 SSD 的计算组织、统计结构、以及硬件执行路径三者对齐，目标是“可部署的 INT8 SSD”。","experiments":"从全文可见的关键信息包括：\n- 速度：在 W4A8 与 W8A8 下，报告最高约 1.4× 推理加速；在单次推理（single-inference）场景下最高约 模型在训练时用两路注入模拟删除：一条在 attention logits 上加 log g，另一条在 values 上乘 g；这样当 g→0 时，token 对后续层的影响会同时在权重和内容两侧消失。推理时再用阈值 τ 把 g<τ 的 KV 物理删除。作者的关键判断是，sigmoid attention 没有 softmax 那种逐行归一化约束，因此 soft gate 对单个 token softmax-gated 模型在同等压缩下反而不如 H2O。作者还做了随机删除 probe，发现 sigmoid 并不是对任意 K/V 删除都更鲁棒；大比例随机删时它反而比 softmax 更敏感。这说明优势来自“可学习删除模式与 attention substrate 的匹配”，不是来自 sigmoid 天生抗删。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 和 contributions, while inference only saves memory when KV entries are physically removed. The question is whether the attention normalization itself affects how well a model can learn a threshold-stable transition from soft gating to hard deletion.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it offers a useful systems-facing insight for model design under inference constraints. The main takeaway is that the attention substrate matters not only for dense LM quality, but also for whether learned cache compression can be made to work cleanly. The result is mildly counterintuitive: sigmoid attention is worse as a dense language model, yet it is a better substrate for learned hard KV eviction. That is relevant for long-context and decode-time efficiency work where training and inference behavior need to align.","technical_approach":"The setup is deliberately controlled: a 2×2×2 comparison over softmax vs sigmoid attention, dense vs learned gating, and RoPE vs NoPE, with the same GPT-2-scale backbone and training setup across all cells. In gated models, deletion is simulated during training through two channels: adding log g to attention logits and multiplying values by g. As g approaches zero, both the weighting path and the content path vanish, making the soft mechanism approximate hard dropping. At inference, entries with g below a threshold are physically removed from the KV cache. The paper’s main hypothesis is that sigmoid attention, because it is not row-normalized like softmax, provides a cleaner local attenuation channel for learned gates and therefore makes threshold-stable deletion easier to learn. To stabilize sigmoid attention norms, the authors use a per-query negative bias b(i) = -log(i+1).","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the Introduction and Method first and keep the controlled variables straight; the paper’s value comes from experimental discipline more than from algorithmic complexity. 在 Apple Neural Engine 上到底什么会被放到 ANE 执行，什么决定了解码速度。作者强调，现有 CoreML 编译计划只能反映编译器“打算”怎么放置，不能证明运行时真的在 ANE 上执行；而常见功耗遥测还会因为芯片通道映射错误给出看似可信但完全错误的零读数。于是论文把问题拆成两个层面：一是 placement，哪些算子表达形式、模型结构、参数规模和权重量化方式会让模型真正驻留在 ANE；二是 decode speed，已经驻留后每 token 的延迟究竟由什么主导。核心结论不是“某个模型更快”，而是 ANE 上的可部署性和速度都强依赖表达形式与字节流量，不能用参数量、FLOPs 或编译器静态计划替代真实测量。","importance":"对基座模型研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它把“硬件感知架构设计”从泛泛而谈推进到一个可操作的结论：在边缘设备上，模型架构不是脱离部署环境独立成立的，尤其在 ANE 这类专用加速器上，算子表达、KV 头分组方式、卷积/注意力混合比例、权重编码精度都会直接改变是否上加速器以及每 token 的字节搬运成本。第二，它给预训练和模型设计一个反直觉但很实用的建议：先选 encoding，再决定把字节预算花在多少参数上，而不是先训练 fp16 模型，最后再把量化当成部署补丁。这个结论对做端侧 LLM、语音 LM、统一多模态小模型的人都很有启发，因为它把“量化”从后处理手段提升成了架构搜索和训练目标的一部分。贡献量级上，这不是新的建模范式，但属于少见的、证据链完整的硬件—模型联合测量研究。","technical_approach":"技术上最重要的是三条彼此独立的测量路径。第一条是 64-shape primitive sweep：作者构造一组保持语义不变、只改变表达形式的 LLM 基本算子实现，逐个记录 supported-device set，而不是只给一个模糊的 residency 分数。这样可以直接证明“placement 是 expression 的属性，不是 semantics 的属性”。文中最典型的例子是 fused RMSNorm 可以 100% eligible，而等价的 subln decomposition 却完全不上 ANE。第二条是 matched trained models：作者不是拿合成权重做微基准，而是训练了一组在结构上严格对齐的真实模型，覆盖不同架构族、规模和精度；量化 checkpoint 与 fp16 版本在结构上 byte-identical，确保部署比较只改变 encoding，不混入图结构差异。第三条是 runtime byte-counter measurement：通过读取 ANE memory-controller 的字节计数器，直接验证推理时到底有没有在 ANE 上跑，以及每 token 实际流过多少字节。这一步很关键，因为它绕开了 CoreML 编译计划和不可靠功耗计量。基于这三条证据，作者提出几个机制判断。其一，placement 由 architecture × size × encoding 共同决定，而不是简单由参数量决定；同样 22.3M 的 attention fp16 可以上 ANE，而 25.9M 的 conv-heavy fp16 可能下不去，但同一 conv 图换成 int8 或 2-bit 后又能大幅回到 ANE。其二，一旦模型驻留，decode 基本表现为“每个 token 流式读取整套权重”，因此速度近似由 streamed bytes/token 决定。其三，在 ANE 上 heavily-grouped GQA，尤其 2 KV heads，并不是人们在 CPU/GPU 直觉里那种昂贵注意力，反而是更小、编译器更愿意接受的算子族；相比之下，LIV conv 这类卷积家族在该硬件上更大、更容易掉出 ANE。也就是说，作者不是在比较 attention 和 conv 的抽象优劣，而是在说明同样的建模自由度在 ANE 上有完全不同的成本曲线。","experiments":"实验部分最有信息量的是几组 headline numbers。首先，placement 方面，fused RMSNorm 与等价分解形式的支持率出现 100% 对 0% 的差异，直接说明表达形式决定设备可执行性。其次，架构与编码的交互上，22.3M 参数的 attention fp16 模型可以在 ANE 上运行，而 25.9M 的 conv-heavy fp16 模型不能；但同一卷积图在 int8/2-bit 编码下可达到约 83% on-ANE residency，这说明参数量不是主变量。第三，作者用硬件计数器测得 decode 的字节成本分别约为 1.539 / 0.753 / 0.197 B/param，对应 fp16 / int8 / 2-bit，和名义上的 2 / 1 / 0.25 B/param 同方向但并不完全相等，说明真实系统里存在额外布局、打包和调度开销，但主导项仍是权重流式搬运。第四，结构比较上，2 KV heads 的 heavily-grouped GQA 规模约为 2.5d^2，而 LIV conv 约为 4d^2，且前者在 fp16 下就能被编译器接受，后者则是唯一明显掉出 ANE 的大类。第五，端到端前沿结果里，ternary 模型占据了 size/speed frontier：约 25M 参数的 ternary 模型在不同 operator mix 下大小约 10.0–10.8 MB、延迟约 0.62–0.64 ms；50M ternary 模型约 16.8 MB、0.86 ms。相对 conv-heavy fp16 起点，作者给出的 headline 改善是 9.8×/6.1× 更小，以及 3.0×/2.2× 更快。值得注意的是，在匹配大小时，operator mix 对 ternary 模型的 size 和 speed 影响已经很小，说明编码杠杆在这个硬件上比算子混合更强。","reading_guide":"这篇适合按“证据链”来读。先看 Introduction 和 Table 1，把五个核心结论记住，再去看对应章节如何支撑。第一优先级是 §4.1 的 primitive sweep，这里最能看清作者为什么坚持“expression 而不是 semantics”这个判断；如果只看结论不看这些对照实验，很容易把它误解成某种编译器偶然性。第二优先级是 §5.1 和 §5.2：前者解释 matched trained artifacts 为什么必要，后者解释为什么必须读 memory-controller byte counters，以及 cross-chip calibration hazard 是怎么出现的。第三优先级是 attention 比 conv 更像小算子。真正值得深挖的点有三个：编译器接受的图形态是否可以被训练时显式约束；字节流量主导是否意味着端侧预训练应直接面向低比特权重；以及这种结论能否迁移到其他 NPU，而不是只停留在 ANE。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a deployment question that sounds simple but had not been answered with trustworthy runtime evidence: what actually gets an LLM onto the Apple Neural Engine, and what determines decode speed once it is there. The authors argue that CoreML compile plans only describe intended placement, not executed placement, and common telemetry can silently fail. They therefore separate the problem into two parts: placement, meaning which operator expressions, model families, sizes, and weight encodings are admitted to ANE execution; and decode cost, meaning what physically dominates per-token latency after residency is achieved. The central claim is not that one architecture is universally faster, but that on ANE both executability and speed are governed by expression form and streamed bytes, not by parameter count, FLOPs, or compiler plans alone.","importance":"For pretraining and foundation-model work, the value is less about Apple-specific deployment and more about hardware-conditioned model design. The paper gives a concrete example of why architecture cannot be chosen independently of the target accelerator. On ANE, operator formulation, grouped attention structure, conv/attention mix, and weight encoding all change whether the model runs on the accelerator at all and how many bytes must be streamed per token. That leads to a practical design rule: choose the encoding first, then spend the byte budget on parameters, instead of training an fp16 model and treating quantization as a late deployment patch. This is especially relevant for edge LLMs, speech LMs, and compact multimodal models where deployment constraints should feed back into pretraining choices.
