con","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22490.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“多语言模型安不安全”，而是更细的一层：同样一套 safety alignment，在不同语言上到底让用户付出了多少额外效用损失，而且这部分损失里有多少是由对齐本身造成的，而不是模型原本的语言能力差异造成的。作者把这个量定义为 Safety Cost，并刻意用“对齐模型 vs 未对齐同源模型”的成对比较来隔离 post-training safety intervention 的净效应。这样做解决了以往多语言安全评测里的一个关键混淆项：如果只看单个对齐模型在不同语言上的绝对表现，无法区分是语言能力弱，还是 safety 机制在该语言上更容易误伤 benign query。论文进一步把关注点放在 over-refusal 场景，即那些表面敏感、但实际安全且应当正常回答的问题，因为只有这类样本既能触发安全机制，又还能定义“有用性下降”这件事。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train 接口研究，这篇工作的价值主要有三点。第一，它把“安全代价”从泛泛的能力退化，收紧成可归因于 safety alignment 的净损失，这个定义更适合做训练闭环分析。第二，它指出多语言安全并不是简单的 coverage 问题，而是一个不对称 trade-off：不少非英语语言同时承受更弱的安全保护和更高的效用损失，也就是作者说的 double penalty。这对多语言对齐数据配比、reward model 覆盖、拒答策略设计都有直接启发。第三，论文强调隐性质量退化同样是安全代价的重要来源，不只是显式拒答。也就是说，即便模型“回答了”，它也可能因为 safety policy 被压缩信息深度、事实精度和操作细节。这一点对只看 refusal rate 的安全评测是个提醒。就贡献量级看，它更像一篇评测与问题定义工作，不直接提出新的训练方法，但它把一个此前被粗略处理的问题拆得更干净，适合作为后续做 multilingual alignment recipe 的诊断基线。","technical_approach":"方法上最重要的是控制变量。作者把 safety alignment 限定为 post-training 阶段的安全相关 SFT、RLHF、DPO 一类干预，不把预训练数据过滤等更早期机制混进来。评测时，对每个 aligned model 找其 unaligned counterpart，用后者作为“无安全约束下的效用参考”。然后在 over-refusal 数据上做直接成对比较，判断 aligned 响应相对 unaligned 响应是否出现了 utility degradation。这个设计比传统“安全集测安全、通用 benign 集测能力”的二分评测更贴近 safety mechanism 的副作用，因为普通 benign benchmark 往往根本触发不了安全过滤器。\n\n从全文描述看，作者不仅看显式拒答，还把“看似合规但信息被削弱”的隐性退化纳入评估。也就是说，比较标准不是简单的答或不答，而是回答是否在信息量、具体性、事实精度上低于未对齐版本。论文还区分了几种表面现象：有些语言看起来 Safety Cost 低，并不代表对齐做得好，而可能只是安全过滤器在该语言上没有真正激活；这种情况下模型既没有很好拦截风险内容，也没有明显损伤 benign utility，于是形成一种虚假的低成本。作者还分析了“达到同等安全水平所需代价”这一层，说明即便是高资源语言，若要把安全性拉到接近英语，也往往需要付出更高 utility cost。这比单看绝对 cost 更有解释力，因为它把安全收益和效用代价放在同一坐标系里。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验核心不是追求大而全 benchmark，而是围绕因果归因做设计。作者使用 over-refusal 任务作为主评测集，覆盖多种语言，并对 aligned / unaligned 模型输出做 pairwise comparison。主要发现有三类。第一，非英语语言整体上承担更高 Safety Cost。第二，多数语言落在 double penalty 区域：安全保护更弱，但 benign utility 损失反而更大。第三，一些语言表面上 utility loss 较小，实则是 safety filter 不活跃，因此不能解读为“更公平”。\n\n论文还做了机制层面的拆分，指出 disparity 不仅来自显式 false refusal，也来自隐性质量下降：即便 aligned 和 unaligned 都给出回答，aligned 版本仍更容易丢失信息深度、事实精度和细节完整性。这个结论很重要，因为它说明只统计 refusal/comply 二元标签会低估安全对齐的真实代价。摘要和正文片段里没有给出完整的模型数、语言数和全部数值表，因此这里不硬填具体数字；但从作者表述看，结论是系统性的，而不是集中在个别低资源语言上的偶发现象。另一个值得注意的结果是，现有 multilingual safety training 并没有消除这种不均衡，说明问题不只是“多加点非英语安全数据”这么简单。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓定义和实验设计，再看结果图。优先看 Introduction 和 Experimental Design，尤其是作者为什么坚持用 aligned vs unaligned 的直接比较，以及为什么选 over-refusal 而不是常规 benign inactive”的讨论，这一点很适合转化成训练和评测中的诊断指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrower and more useful question than standard multilingual safety evaluation: how much utility loss is caused by safety alignment itself in each language, after separating it from pre-existing language capability gaps. The authors call this quantity Safety Cost. Their main methodological move is to compare a safety-aligned model directly against its unaligned counterpart, so the measured gap is attributable to post-training safety interventions rather than to weaker baseline proficiency in a given language. They focus on over-refusal settings, where prompts look sensitive enough to trigger safety mechanisms but are actually benign and should receive a helpful answer. That setting makes utility loss measurable in a way that ordinary benign benchmarks usually cannot.","importance":"$4b","technical_approach":"$4c","experiments":"$4d","reading_guide":"For a skim read, start with the problem setup and the evaluation protocol. We propose DIAG, a Diagnostic Iterative Alignment and Generation framework th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22806.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是在线数学偏好蒸馏里的一个数据效率问题：随着 student 变强，固定题库越来越不匹配当前能力边界，导致 rollouts 不是太容易就是太难，最终拿不到足够多“一个对、一个错”的自然偏好对。DPO 或其他 preference optimization 在数学任务上往往受限于这种 signal scarcity，而不是单纯受限于优化器或模型容量。DIAG 试图把这个问题显式建模成 practice distribution 的控制问题：每一轮训练后先诊断当前题目分布还能产出多少有效 preference pairs，再据此调整下一轮题目主题和难度，让生成集中在 student 的决策边界附近。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 pretrain-posttrain 接口有现实意义。它提醒人注意一个常被忽略的事实：在线对齐或推理蒸馏的瓶颈，很多时候不是缺 teacher，也不是缺算力，而是缺“信息密度足够高的训练样本”。这个视角和持续预训练、mid-training、reasoning data synthesis 是相通的。若把数学偏好蒸馏换成更一般的能力蒸馏，DIAG 代表的是一种 signal-aware curriculum：不是盲目扩大数据池，而是根据当前模型错误分布去生成下一轮数据。这个思路对合成预训练数据、continual pretrain 中的数据配比、以及 RL/SFT 阶段如何避免样本过易或过难，都有启发。不过它仍然偏 post-train，且场景限定在可验证数学推理，外推到开放域任务时需要谨慎。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Diagnose-and-Generate 两阶段闭环。第一阶段 Diagnose & Allocate，目标是估计当前 practice distribution 的“有效监督产率”，也就是给定一批题目和当前 student，多大概率能采到既有正确轨迹又有错误轨迹的 rollout 集合，从而形成可用 preference pair。作者没有把 topic-level 统计直接拿来做硬决策，而是引入 Empirical Bayes shrinkage 来平滑低样本主题的估计，避免某些主题因为偶然几次高收益就被过度放大，或者因为样本太少被过早放弃。与此同时，系统会在 exploration 和 exploitation 之间做配额分配：一部分预算继续覆盖更广主题，防止课程塌缩；另一部分预算集中到当前高产率区域。\n\n第二阶段是 mistake-conditioned generation。与其从静态候选池里做 active selection，DIAG 让 teacher 根据 student 最近的错误轨迹生成后续题目。这样生成出来的问题更贴近 student 当前失败模式，也更可能落在“差一点会、但还没完全会”的区域。文中还提到 uncertainty-weighted resampling，用来把计算预算进一步压到决策边界附近，而不是平均撒在所有主题上。\n\n这个方法和传统 active learning 的区别在于，它不是从现成题库里挑高不确定样本，而是根据 student 的错误结构主动生成新题；和 brute-force 扩大 rollout width 的区别在于，它优先提高每个样本产生有效 preference signal 的概率，而不是单纯多采样。若从训练信号角度看，DIAG 关心的是 pair yield 和 pair quality 两件事：前者决定监督密度，后者决定偏好学习是否真的贴着能力边界发生。","experiments":"从已给出的全文内容看，实验重点不是追求单次 benchmark 的绝对分数，而是证明 DIAG 在相同或更低预算下能产出更多有效 preference pairs，并把这些更高信息密度的样本转化为更好的数学推理性能。引言里明确声称：静态 practice distribution 会导致 natural contrastive supervision 快速下降；DIAG 通过 signal-aware pacing、Empirical Bayes topic scoring 和错误条件生成，提高了 preference-pair yield，并且单位预算的信息密度更高。\n\n由于当前提供的正文片段没有完整实验表和具体数字，能确认的实验结论主要是方向性的：一，随着迭代进行，固定题库越来越难提供有效 pair；二，按 topic 做稳健配额分配优于直接按原始统计贪心采样；三，mistake-conditioned generation 比 practice-agnostic generation 更能贴近 student 的能力边界，因此更容易形成有区分度的偏好监督。若阅读全文，建议重点核对三类数字：每轮有效 pair 比例、总训练预算下的最终数学 benchmark 提升、以及不同 topic allocation 策略下的方差和稳定性。没有这些数字，很难判断它到底是“更省数据”还是只是“更会造题”。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住一个主线：这不是在改 DPO 目标，而是在改 DPO 根据 student 错误生成新题时，是否会逐渐收缩到狭窄题型，造成泛化下降；三，Empirical Bayes 的先验设定对 topic allocation 有多敏感；四，pair yield 提高后，最终收益来自更好的 curriculum，还是来自 teacher 生成题目本身更接近测试分布。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a data-efficiency bottleneck in online mathematical preference distillation. As the student improves, a fixed prompt pool becomes increasingly mismatched to the model’s competence boundary, so rollouts are often either too easy or too hard. In both cases, the system fails to produce enough natural preference pairs with one correct and one incorrect trajectory. DIAG reframes this as a curriculum-control problem: after each alignment round, diagnose how much usable preference signal the current practice distribution still yields, then reshape the next round of practice so that generation stays near the student’s decision boundary.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface. The paper highlights a practical point that often gets missed: in online alignment and reasoning distillation, the bottleneck is frequently not teacher quality or raw compute, but the density of informative supervision. That perspective transfers to synthetic data generation, continual pretraining, mid-training curricula, and reasoning-focused post-training. In a broader sense, DIAG is a signal-aware curriculum method: instead of scaling the prompt pool blindly, it adapts future data generation to the student’s observed failure modes. We argue for an alternative that breaks the dilemma by moving regularization to the input side. Standard PPO/GRPO/RLHF recipes rely on an action-side Policy-KL against a reference model. This stabilizes response updates, but it also consumes part of the exploration budget in response space. Removing it leaves the run exposed to drift and oscillation. The authors identify another source of instability: the model-induced likelihood of the training queries themselves changes as the policy is updated. Even with a fixed prompt corpus, the model’s autoregressive probability over these prompts drifts, creating an input-side non-stationarity that is not controlled by response-level KL.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented research is the input-side view of post-training dynamics. RL updates do not only change answer behavior; they also reshape the model’s likelihood landscape over prompts and prefixes. That matters for continual pretraining, annealing, RLVR data selection, and the pretrain–posttrain interface. Moving part of the regularization from response space to query space may preserve more room for answer exploration while still controlling drift. The method itself is a moderate extension to existing RL pipelines, but the diagnostic framing is useful for studying collapse, high-temperature robustness, and prompt-mixture sensitivity in reasoning RL.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is ERPO. It defines a policy-induced query distribution ρ_θ(q)=P_θ(q), where the query is treated as a self-terminating autoregressive token sequence under the current model. The pre-RL model gives the reference distribution ρ_θ0. ERPO adds a Query-KL penalty, KL(ρ_θ || ρ_θ0), to limit drift in this input-side distribution. This differs from Policy-KL, which constrains π_θ(o|q), the response distribution conditioned on a query. A second component is a reference-derived, dataset-static per-query weight. Queries that are more typical under the reference distribution receive more stable update weights, which is intended to reduce variance and improve behavior under high-temperature sampling. The implementation is shown on top of GRPO, but the components are designed to be compatible with PPO, REINFORCE-style estimators, RLOO, and related advantage-based objectives.","experiments":"The provided text reports the main empirical pattern but not the full numerical tables. The key diagnostic result is that, under a fixed action-level Policy-KL budget, response-level Policy-KL remains roughly flat while the estimated Query-KL keeps increasing over training. This supports the claim that action-side regularization does not control input-side drift. The authors evaluate on reasoning benchmarks and report consistent accuracy gains for ERPO. They also use multi-temperature sampling with Pass@k, Pass@1, and Avg@k, which is appropriate because high-temperature decoding exposes long-tail instability. The visible text does not specify model sizes, data scale, benchmark names, or exact improvement margins, so the strength of the empirical claim should be checked in the full result tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. Start with Figure 1 in the introduction, since the divergence between Query-KL and Policy-KL is the central motivation. Then read the definition of ρ_θ(q)=P_θ(q) in the preliminaries and decide whether this notion maps cleanly to the RL pipeline you use. In the method section, focus on the exact Query-KL estimator and the reference-derived per-query weights: whether they require extra reference forward passes, how they interact with advantage normalization, and whether Policy-KL is removed or combined. In the experiments, look for ablations separating QKL from query weighting and for results at different decoding temperatures. A useful question to keep in mind is whether regularizing prompt likelihood truly stabilizes the environment, or whether it constrains general language-model parameters in a way that may trade off with adaptation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看目标函数定义和梯度路径；若 QKL setup 干净，它会改变“KL 只能绑 response”的默认做法。","en":"Read the objective and gradient-flow design; if the QKL setup is clean, it weakens the default assumption that KL control must live on responses."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xianlei Zhou","Xiangdi Meng","Yu He","Tianyu Qi","Shuyan Guan","Xianli Zhang","Jian Zhang","Xin Li","Qika Lin","Jun Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLHF","policy-optimization","KL-regularization","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23311","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.23311","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.23497","title":"Mitigating Reasoning-Induced Misalignment via Safety-Direction Penalty","abstract":"Reasoning-Induced Misalignment, where fine-tuning on reasoning data containing no harmful content, including mathematics, code, and problem-solving with chain-of-thought traces can induce harmful behaviors of LLM, posing a serious challenge to the safety of LLM reasoning. unnormal","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_23566.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、但在 LLM RL 里长期没有被认真拆开的难点：单 rollout 的 actor-critic 训练为什么总是不稳，以及怎样把 critic 真正做成比 GRPO 更省样本、又不更脆弱的方案。作者指出，现有 critic-based recipe 的问题不是单一组件失效，而是几处设计彼此错配：PPO 的 ratio clipping 对高低概率 token 约束不均；bootstrap value target 会把 critic 自身误差再喂回训练；固定 λ 的 GAE 在不同长度回答上会让终局奖励权重差异很大；线性 value head 可以预测到 reward 支持集之外；batch 内 advantage normalization 会在人为固定尺度的同时抹掉“策略残差信号已经变小”这一事实。论文的核心任务，就是把这些不匹配逐项拆开，给出一套在 outcome-only reward 分数本身。","technical_approach":"$52","experiments":"$53","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读但要抓住几个关键段落。\n\n先看 Introduction，作者把 instability 来源拆成哪几类，问题定义很清楚。然后直接看 controlled sanity test 对应章节和图表，这部分最能说明每个 design choice 为什么存在。若时间有限，优先看：标准 PPO vs DPPO、bounded vs unbounded value head、MC target vs bootstrap target、normalized vs raw advantage 这几组对照。\n\n接着看方法部分里对 length-adaptive GAE 的定义。这里不是公式花样，而是在 outcome-only reward 下重新校准 credit assignment 的长度偏置。若你平时做 RLHF/GRPO，值得把它和固定 λ 的终局奖励传播曲线对照着看。\n\n最后看 privileged critic input 的实验。重点不是分数高低，而是理解为什么“训练时可见、推理时不可见”的信息可以合法地喂给 critic，以及它何时会带来 actor 侧收益、何时只是在拟合 value。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主要内容；若要复现实验或迁移到 reasoning RL，建议把附录里的实现细节和超参也看完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a specific but important failure mode in LLM RL: why single-rollout actor-critic training is often unstable, and how to make a critic-based recipe competitive with group-based methods such as GRPO. The authors argue that the issue is not one broken component but a stack of mismatches: PPO ratio clipping treats low- and high-probability tokens unevenly; bootstrapped value targets recycle critic error; a fixed GAE lambda propagates terminal reward very differently across short and long responses; an unconstrained linear value head predicts outside the known reward support; and batch-wise advantage normalization keeps amplifying noise even when the true policy signal has already shrunk. The paper’s central goal is to realign these pieces into a critic training recipe that is stable in outcome-only reward settings common in LLM reasoning RL.","importance":"Its main contribution is not a new RL algorithm class, but a practical answer to a growing question in post-training and RL-pretrain interfaces: do we really need multi-rollout group estimators to get stable learning signals for LLMs? If a critic can be trained reliably, one rollout per prompt becomes viable, token-level credit assignment becomes sharper, and privileged training-time information can be used without changing deployment-time policy inputs. That matters for sample efficiency, for reasoning RL where rollout cost is high, and for understanding whether current preference for GRPO-style methods is algorithmic or simply a consequence of brittle critic recipes. The paper is most useful as a training-dynamics study with a reproducible recipe, not as a benchmark-chasing result.","technical_approach":"$54","experiments":"$55","reading_guide":"$56","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 recipe 消融就够；若结果稳，内部 RLVR 应复现 bounded value 和 rubric-conditioned critic。","en":"Read the recipe ablations; if stable, bounded values and rubric-conditioned critics deserve an internal RLVR replication."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Penghui Qi","Xiangxin Zhou","Wee Sun Lee"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLVR","critic_training","GRPO","mathematical_reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23566","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.23566","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.21595","title":"Perturb the Thought, Not the Pixels: Latent-Space Rollout Diversification for Reinforcement Learning of Vision-Language Models","abstract":"Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning ability of vision-language models (VLMs), and diversifying the rollouts within each optimization group amplifies its gains. Existing approaches diversify through decoding temperature or pixel-space image distortion; we ask whether the perturbation belongs in the model's latent space instead. We introduce Noise-Contrastive GRPO (NC-GRPO), which injects scale-calibrated Gaussian noise into the last hidden layer of the pro","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21595.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 RLVR 里一个常被当作实现细节、其实直接决定学习信号强弱的问题：组内 rollout 的多样性应该从哪里来。GRPO 依赖同一 prompt 下多条回答的相对奖励；如果组内样本高度一致，优势信号就会塌掉。已有做法主要靠 decoding temperature，或者像 NoisyRollout 那样在图像像素空间加噪声。作者要解决的是：这些扰动都没有直接作用在模型内部的“问题表征”上，能否把分叉点前移到 latent space，让 rollout 在隐藏状态层面分化，从而得到更有用的组内对比信号。","importance":"对基座研究的直接相关性不如纯预训练论文强，但它对 post-train 阶段的 rollout 设计有一个清楚的启发：在 group-based RL 中，真正重要的不是表面上的采样随机性，而是能否制造对策略有区分度的内部状态分叉。这个思路和 latent reasoning、hidden-state perturbation、以及 test-time branching 有天然联系。若以后把 RL 信号更深地接到预训练或 mid-training 阶段，latent-space diversification 可能比输入空间扰动更通用，因为它不依赖具体模态，也不要求额外的数据增强管线。","technical_approach":"方法叫 NC-GRPO（Noise-Contrastive GRPO）。做法很简单：在每个 rollout group 中，只对一半分支在 prompt encoding 的 prefill 阶段注入高斯噪声，而且噪声加在最后一层 hidden state 上；另一半分支保持标准 GRPO。这样，同一 prompt 会从两个不同的内部起点出发生成回答。若某些 noisy branch 在被扰动后仍能走到正确答案，它们就会在组内相对奖励下被强化；若被扰动后偏离正确轨道，则会被压低。于是，模型对这一“分叉点扰动”的敏感性被转成了 policy gradient 信号。\n\n关键设计有几处。\n\n第一，噪声只加在 prefill forward，不在 decode 过程中持续注入。作者明确区分了“改变 rollout 出发状态”和“改变生成时持续可见的缓存表示”这两件事。NC-GRPO 只做前者，因此 decode 成本与标准 GRPO 相同，也更容易把效果归因到初始 latent branching，而不是整个生成过程被噪声污染。\n\n第二，噪声尺度按 hidden norm 校准。具体做法是让扰动的期望二范数与原 hidden state 范数成比例，因此 σ 是无量纲、对 hidden width 不敏感的相对扰动强度。文中还给出方差随维度 d 以 2/d 收缩的性质，说明在高维表示里，实际扰动幅度会集中在设定值附近。这个设计避免了“不同模型宽度下同一 σ 含义不同”的问题。\n\n第三，噪声采用 annealing schedule，早期探索更强，后期逐步降到 0。这和 NoisyRollout 的训练节奏一致，但这里扰动对象从像素换成了 latent state。\n\n第四，作者做了 antithetic 和 steered probes。前者用相反方向的噪声对，后者用可控方向扰动，目的是区分“只是加了噪声预算”与“独立随机分叉”哪个才是有效因素。结论是后者更关键：真正起作用的是独立 stochastic diversity，而不是某个特定方向或总噪声能量。\n\n从方法定位看，它不是在改 GRPO objective，也不是在改 reward model，而是在改 rollout 生成分布。这个切入点比较干净，因为训练目标、评测方式、解码设置都尽量保持不变，便于把收益归因到 diversification 机制本身。","experiments":"实验主要在多模态数学推理和幻觉鲁棒性上展开。作者报告，在五个 multimodal math OOD benchmark 上，NC-GRPO 相比 vanilla GRPO 有显著提升，pooled McNemar 检验的显著性范围是 p=0.001 到 p<1e-4。除了 OOD，文中也说 in-domain accuracy 有提升，说明 latent perturbation 不只是做 domain shift regularization。\n\n与图像空间噪声相比，NC-GRPO 在 hallucination robustness 上更好：POPE 上显著，p=0.007；HallusionBench 提升 0.6 个百分点。这个结果有两层含义。其一，latent diversification 并不只是让答案更散，而是可能让模型学到对内部表征扰动更稳健的决策边界。其二，像素噪声会把变化限制在视觉编码器能表达的方向上，而 latent 噪声更直接地作用于跨模态融合后的内部状态。\n\n机制实验是这篇文章最值得看的部分。作者用 matched antithetic 和 steered probes 说明，收益主要来自独立随机分叉，而不是固定方向扰动或更大的噪声预算。另一个有用结果是 noise-scale study：σ0 控制 specialization 程度，较大噪声会更偏向 reasoning gain、但可能牺牲 general capability；文中指出 σ0=0.2 是一个较平衡的区间，在 reasoning 上有收益，同时 general capability 没有可测下降。\n\n摘要和正文片段没有给出完整模型规模、训练步数和所有 benchmark 的逐项分数，因此这里不宜编造具体数字。可以确认的硬结果是：五个 OOD 多模态数学 benchmark 上显著优于 vanilla GRPO；POPE 上 p=0.007；HallusionBench +0.6；并做了不同噪声尺度与机制探针实验。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点放在方法动机和机制验证，不必把它当成一个需要逐式推导的 RL 论文。\n\n先看 Introduction 和 Method 3.1，弄清楚作者到底扰动了什么、没有扰动什么。尤其要注意：噪声只在 prefill 的最后一层 hidden state 注入，decode 时不继续加噪，也不改 objective。这一点决定了实验解释是否干净。\n\n然后看噪声尺度校准公式和对应命题。这里的价值不在数学难度，而在它把 σ 变成了 width-invariant modality-agnostic 的论证以及实现开销描述。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical but underexamined question in RL with verifiable rewards: where should rollout diversity come from? GRPO relies on within-group reward differences, so if all rollouts for a prompt are too similar, the learning signal collapses. Existing diversification methods mainly use decoding temperature or image-space perturbations such as noisy images. The paper asks whether the perturbation should instead be applied directly to the model’s internal representation, so that rollouts branch from different latent states rather than merely from different pixel inputs or sampling randomness.","importance":"Its relevance to core pretraining is indirect, but the paper offers a useful lesson for post-training and potentially for mid-training RL: what matters in group-based RL is not superficial sample randomness, but whether the policy is exposed to informative internal-state branching. That connects naturally to latent reasoning, hidden-state perturbation, and branching-based exploration. If RL signals are pushed earlier into the training pipeline, latent-space diversification may be more general than input-space augmentation because it is modality-agnostic and does not require a separate augmentation stack for each modality.","technical_approach":"$57","experiments":"$58","reading_guide":"$59","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将探索噪声从 decoding/pixel 移到 latent space，对 RLVR 训练 VLM 的多样性生成有启发，值得关注其 noise scale 的设定和梯度回传机制。","en":"Moving exploration noise from decoding/pixel to latent space offers insights for diverse generation in RLVR for VLMs; worth checking the noise scale setup and gradient flow mechanism."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Michael Jerge","Joseph Pelczar","Justin Downes"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Vision-Language Models","Reinforcement Learning","Latent Space Perturbation","GRPO","Mathematical Reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21595","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21595","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.21883","title":"VIG: Visual Information Gain as a Reward Signal for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Compression","abstract":"Multimodal large reasoning models often rely on long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces in which a substantial fraction of tokens, such as repeated visual descriptions, self-reflection, and other visually-disengaged filler, inflate inference cost without contributing to the answer. Existing CoT compression methods optimize output length but never measure whether a reasoning token is actually grounded in the image. We propose \\textbf{VIG} (Visual Information Gain), an information-theoretic GRPO reward","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21883.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多模态推理模型在长链式思维中的一个具体失真：模型会生成大量并不依赖图像的“伪视觉推理”token，例如重复描述题面、泛化代数步骤、自我反思和来回修正。这类token一方面显著抬高推理成本，另一方面会把模型从最初正确的视觉读取带偏，形成 over-reasoning。已有压缩方法大多只惩罚长度，或者依赖压缩后的参考链，却没有回答一个更关键的问题：某个推理token到底是否真的由图像支撑。论文把这个问题形式化为“图像对该token预测不确定性的降低量”，即逐token的 Visual Information Gain，并据此训练模型偏向高视觉密度的推理链，而不是单纯更短的推理链。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于又做了一个 CoT 压缩技巧，而在于给多模态推理引入了一个可在线计算、无需外部标注的 grounding reward。这个奖励把“是否看图”从模糊的行为描述，变成了可优化的训练信号。它的贡献有三层。第一，方法层面，它把多模态 CoT 压缩从 length control 改写成 information density optimization，这个视角比长度惩罚更稳，因为它区分了必要视觉token和语言填充token。第二，训练层面，它只用同一策略模型做两次前向，一次带图像、一次去掉视觉token，不需要奖励模型、参考答案链或人工步骤标注，适合接到现有 GRPO 流程里。第三，研究层面，它提示多模态 RL 不一定要围绕最终答案或偏好建奖励，也可以围绕中间推理的跨模态依赖结构建奖励。这对后续做 grounded reasoning、视觉-语言 credit assignment、甚至统一多模态 pretrain/post-train 接口都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把每个推理位置 τ 上 token tτ 对图像的依赖，写成条件互信息意义下的量。实现时并不显式估计互信息，而是用同一策略在两种条件下对该位置的预测熵差来近似：一种条件包含问题、历史上下文和图像；另一种条件去掉图像，仅保留文本上下文。若加入图像后该位置的预测分布明显更确定，说明这个token更依赖视觉证据，VIG 分数更高；若两次前向差异很小，则该token大概率是语言上下文就能推出的 filler。论文将所有 <think> token 的逐步 VIG 聚合成序列级奖励，再与标准的格式奖励、答案正确性奖励一起送入 GRPO。\n\n这里有几个设计点值得注意。第一，它不直接奖励短输出，因此不会像 length penalty 那样把“必要但长”的视觉推理也一并压掉。链长缩短是优化视觉信息密度后的副产物。第二，它不依赖外部教师链，因此避免了蒸馏式压缩常见的 teacher bias，也不需要做 step segmentation。第三，奖励是 online 计算的，和当前策略同步变化，这比离线打分更贴近真实 policy distribution。第四，论文强调用 entropy reduction 而不是 token log-prob 差值，原因是前者更关注图像是否降低了该位置整体不确定性，而不是只奖励当前采样token本身的偶然概率抬升。\n\n从方法边界看，这仍然是一个 post-training / RL 阶段的方法，不是预训练目标替换。但它提出的信号形式很像一种可泛化的跨模态 credit assignment：判断中间表示是否真的使用了某个模态，而不是只看最终答案是否正确。这一点比具体的 GRPO 配方更值得记。","experiments":"实验覆盖三种 Qwen3-VL-Thinking 尺寸和六个主多模态推理 benchmark，并额外在 R1-Onevision-Bench 上做泛化评测。论文主结果是效率—准确率 Pareto 改善，而不是只报一个平均分。以 Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking 为例，VIG 达到 64.92% 平均准确率，效率指标 ACC per 1k tokens 为 52.42；相对最强压缩基线 ThinkPrune，准确率提升 2.37 个百分点，效率提升 39.4%。文中还给出一个很直观的案例：基础模型在一个简单几何题上生成 823 个 token，最后答错；L1 长度惩罚把链压到 412 token 并答对；VIG 只用 105 token 就完成正确求解，说明它不是单纯截断，而是更早抓住了图像中的关键证据。\n\n从论文叙述看，消融重点主要围绕三件事。第一，和 plain GRPO、L1、ThinkPrune、TALE 等方法比较，验证“视觉依赖奖励”优于“纯文本压缩信号”。第二，跨模型尺寸保持在 Pareto frontier 上，说明收益不是某个单一模型的偶然。第三，在 R1-Onevision-Bench 上总体排名第一，并在五个学科类别中三个第一或并列第一，说明该奖励并不只适配主 benchmark 的题型。\n\n虽然给出的全文片段没有完整表格，但可以确认作者把收益拆成两部分：一部分是 token 数显著下降，另一部分是答案准确率不降反升。这一点很关键，因为很多压缩方法只能换来更低成本，却牺牲正确率。这里的结果支持作者关于 over-reasoning 会伤害多模态正确性的判断。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看引言里的失败案例和 Figure 2，理解作者为什么认为多模态长 CoT 的问题不是“太长”而是“很多 token 没在看图”。第二，看方法部分对 VIG 的定义和 two-pass 计算流程，重点理解为什么用“带图/去图”的熵差来做代理信号，以及它和直接长度惩罚的区别。第三，看主结果图，尤其是 accuracy-efficiency Pareto 图，而不是只看平均准确率。\n\n如果时间有限，建议阅读顺序是：Introduction → Method 3.1/3.2 → 主实验表和 Pareto 图 → ablation。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以把主要思想读清。更值得深挖的点有两个：一是 entropy-based VIG 是否会偏好某些词类或模板化视觉描述；二是 two-pass reward 的训练开销和方差控制如何影响可扩展性。如果后续想把这个思路迁移到更底层训练，可以思考它能否变成 pretrain 阶段的 masked grounding objective，或者作为多模态 token-level filtering 信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in multimodal reasoning models: long chains of thought contain many tokens that are not actually grounded in the image. These include question restatements, generic algebra, self-reflection, and repeated visual narration. Such tokens are expensive at inference time, but the more important point is that they can hurt correctness by letting the model drift away from the original visual evidence. Existing compression methods mostly optimize for shorter outputs, or rely on compressed reference traces, without asking whether a reasoning token truly depends on the image. This work turns that missing question into a token-level training signal: how much the image reduces uncertainty about the next reasoning token.","importance":"The main contribution is not just another CoT compression recipe. It introduces a grounded reward for multimodal reasoning that is online, self-contained, and does not require external annotations or reward models. That matters for three reasons. First, it reframes multimodal CoT compression from length minimization to visual information density maximization, which is a better fit for the actual problem. Second, it provides a practical RL signal that can be dropped into GRPO with only two forward passes of the same policy, avoiding teacher traces and auxiliary supervision. Third, it suggests a broader direction for multimodal training: reward intermediate reasoning based on cross-modal dependence, not only final-answer correctness. For researchers working on grounded reasoning, multimodal credit assignment, or the pretrain-to-post-train interface, that shift in training signal is the interesting part.","technical_approach":"$5a","experiments":"$5b","reading_guide":"$5c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看奖励定义和消融：用带/不带图像两次前向做 token 级 grounding，setup 干净，适合复用到内部 VLM RL。","en":"Read the reward definition and ablations: token-level grounding from paired with/without-image passes is a clean setup worth trying in internal VLM RL."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Wen Luo","Xiaohan Yi","Xiaotao Huang","Liqun Huang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multimodal-reasoning","GRPO","CoT-compression","reward-design"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21883","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21883","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.21501","title":"Let Credit Follow Computation: Architecture-Aware Credit Transport for Large Language Model Reinforcement Learning","abstract":"Credit assignment in large-language-model reinforcement learning (LLM RL) can be separated into three objects: evidence about success, a transport operator that converts this evidence into token-level advantages, and an update geometry that turns advantages into policy changes. Recent work has greatly improved evidence, sampling, and update geometry, but the transport operator is usually architecture-agnostic. Fixed-discount GAE applies a stationary geometric kernel along token time; group-relat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21501.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文关注 LLM 强化学习中的 credit transport：延迟奖励或过程奖励如何被分配到 token-level advantages，而不是只改 verifier、sampling 或 PPO/GRPO 的 update geometry。","importance":"启发点在于把 LLM RL 训练信号拆成三层：credit evidence、credit transport、update geometry。很多 reasoning RL 工作主要改证据来源或更新几何，但默认用固定折扣 GAE 或 response-level broadcast。论文指出 Transformer 的实际计算依赖是 trajectory-specific 的，credit assignment 不一定应该沿 token 时间轴使用固定几何核。这对 RL post-train 乃至 RL-for-pretrain 有参考价值：训练信号质量不只取决于 reward，也取决于 reward 如何沿模型实际计算路径传播。","technical_approach":"论文提出 computation-conditioned credit transport，简称 CCT。标准 GAE 可看成一个固定的上三角时间核，权重只依赖 token 距离；GRPO 一类 group-relative 方法则近似把 response-level statistic 广播到整个回复。CCT 改为从 behavior policy 的内部计算中抽取一个 detached statistic，并通过有界映射得到 retention gate κ_t。之后 advantage trace 中从后续 residual δ_u 传到前面位置 t 的权重，是中间 gates 的路径乘积。因此 transport kernel 变成非平稳、样本相关，并由旧策略的内部计算结构条件化。具体算法 CompPO 使用 attention concentration 作为廉价的 policy-native 结构信号：当当前位置注意力集中在少数历史 token 上时，credit trace 和 bootstrap 的保留方式不同于注意力分散时。论文还引入 transport-aligned critic，利用 actor hidden states 和 attention 对 value-relevant history 做 pooling，并用同一 gate 连接局部 value 与 routed-history value。外层仍使用 clipped PPO，verifier、rollout 格式和 reward 不变。一个重要边界是：attention 不直接乘 policy gradient；它只参数化 bootstrap 系数、GAE trace 权重和 critic state geometry。","experiments":"给出的全文片段只展示了实验设计意图，没有暴露完整任务、模型规模和具体数值。可确认的是，实验被设计为排除几个替代解释：CCT 不是简单的 attention-weighted loss，也不声称 attention 是因果归因；attention concentration 只是 policy-native structural proxy，需要通过 controlled alignment tests 验证。论文的实验重点应是比较固定折扣 GAE、response-level broadcast / group-relative credit 与 computation-conditioned trace，在相同 PPO 外层、相同 verifier 和相同 reward 下的差异。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30 到 45 分钟。重点看 introduction 中三分法：credit evidence、credit transport、update geometry；这能帮助判断它与 delayed or process-level evidence is converted into token-level advantages. It separates this issue from reward construction, sampling, and PPO/GRPO-style update geometry.","importance":"The useful idea is the decomposition of an LLM RL pipeline into credit evidence, credit transport, and update geometry. Many recent reasoning-RL methods improve verifiers, baselines, sampling, clipping, or normalization, while leaving transport as either fixed-discount GAE or response-level broadcasting. The paper argues that this creates an architecture-credit mismatch: a Transformer uses trajectory-specific dependencies, but the credit estimator often uses a stationary token-time kernel. This is relevant for post-training and for RL-style pretraining objectives because reward quality is not only about the reward source; it also depends on how evidence is propagated through the model’s own computation.","technical_approach":"$5d","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt gives the experimental intent but not complete task details, model sizes, or numeric results. RL 失效点：在视觉-语言数学推理里，如果基础模型初始成功率很低，GRPO 这类组内相对优势方法会因为“整组全错”而拿不到有效梯度，训练几乎处于饥饿状态。作者不是再提一个单点技巧，而是把三类常见补救思路放到同一控制条件下比较：文本先验（hint）、分布先验（teacher distillation）、价值先验（critic）。论文真正要回答的问题不是“哪种方法名字更好”，而是“先验是否真的传递到了 policy”，以及在极端 sparse-reward 条件下，哪些设计能把无梯度状态改造成可学习状态。","importance":"对预训练研究者来说，这篇文章的价值不在 GUI 或 VLM 任务本身，而在它把 post-train RL 中一个常被模糊处理的问题拆开了：当 reward 太稀疏时，训练成败首先取决于训练信号能否穿透到 token 级更新，而不是取决于目标函数表面形式。这个结论对 reasoning RL、RLVR、以及更广义的 pretrain↔post-train 接口都有参考意义。第二个价值点是评测有效性：作者发现一个长期使用的 held-out 集合会系统性奖励“模型没变”，甚至与真实跨域迁移负相关，这对任何依赖固定验证集做 recipe 选择的训练工作都很重要。第三个价值点是工程验证：评分器、答案抽取器、judge rollout group 全错。然后在完全相同的训练预算、数据池、随机种子和硬件条件下，比较 11 个训练臂。文本先验部分，核心是给模型前缀一段参考解法，让采样从更接近成功的状态开始；作者还区分了“hint 真正改善探索”与“UFT 类辅助似然项是否额外有用”，结论是收益主要来自探索起点改变，而不是额外的监督项。分布先验部分，作者比较了多种 on-policy distillation 方式，关键差别在于 teacher 信号是否被 teacher 能力上限卡住、是否只在失败样本上 gated、以及是否随训练退火；结果显示 annealed global distillation 有效，而 failure-gated persistent distillation 不行，说明蒸馏信号如果只在局部失败区域触发，往往送不到真正决定策略迁移的地方。价值先验部分，作者训练 critic 给 token 级 credit assignment，但重点不是“有没有 critic”，而是 critic 的参数化是否正确：把 clipped-MSE 标量回归换成 HL-Gauss categorical loss 之后，credit 传播质量明显改善。作者把这个变化描述为一行替换，但含义很重：在稀疏奖励下，value head 的表示形式会直接决定先验是否能被 policy 吸收。最后还测试了 text prior 和 distribution prior 叠加，发现几乎没有叠加收益，说明二者主要都在解决同一个早期瓶颈，而不是互补瓶颈。","experiments":"实验设计很干净。11 个 arms 用同一训练池、同一步数和 batch 预算、同一 seed、每个 arm 一台 8×A800 节点。评测采用独立 LLM judge（DeepSeek）盲评完整输出，并用 McNemar exact test 做成对显著性检验。核心结果有三层。第一层是“先验是否送达”的分层：在训练域外的 DynaMath 上，6 个先验确实传到 policy 的方法集中在 30.6%–32.9%，其余 5 个方法——包括 plain GRPO 和几种先验被 teacher cap、gating 或 critic 失配吞掉的变体——集中在 27.0%–29.1%，两组区间不重叠。第二层是组件级归因：hint 预验证可把成功率从 7.2% 拉到 34.4%，说明探索起点确实能解锁稀疏任务；critic 从 clipped-MSE 改成 HL-Gauss 后，in-domain accuracy 提升 +14.4 个百分点，并把原本最弱的训练臂变成第二好的 cross-domain 臂；双先验叠加没有额外收益。第三层是评测集诊断：旧的 held-out 集 old-319 与真实跨域迁移排名呈负相关，Spearman ρ=-0.74；最难的 in-domain 子集 sparse-800 与迁移呈强正相关，ρ=+0.89。作者进一步分析 old-319 中 69% 是四选一选择题，各方法都接近 25% 猜测下限，因此“保持输出短选项字母”的旧行为反而拿高分，导致它奖励模型不发生能力变化。论文还做了 scoring audit，发现启发式答案抽取器会对不同方法造成最高 30 个百分点的系统偏差，甚至能颠倒排序；同时排查了 guessing 和 format reward hacking，认为主结果不是这些伪因素造成的。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction solves a problem, most rollout groups are entirely wrong, the group-relative advantage becomes zero, and training receives no useful gradient. The real question is not which family of tricks sounds better, but which kind of prior actually reaches the policy under extreme sparse-reward conditions. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21376.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很具体但容易被忽略的问题：在带引用的科学问答偏好数据中，人类和 LLM judge 到底如何看待 citation。核心不是判断答案是否事实正确，而是分析 citation count、citation diversity、citation age、citation mismatch 等可解释特征，会怎样影响 pairwise preference。这个问题直接关系到 RAG、deep research 和 reward model 训练，因为偏好标签可能把“引用形式”误当成“回答质量”。","importance":"对预训练本身不是一篇核心论文，但对 post-train 数据质量和 reward modeling 有清晰启发。偏好数据里 citation 是一个强混杂因素：模型可能学到“多引用更可信”或“少而分散的引用更好”这类表面规则，而不是学到 grounded reasoning。论文的价值在于把 citation 从一个展示格式问题，转成了可量化的 preference bias 问题。对于训练研究型助手、RAG judge、citation-aware reward model，这类分析可以帮助设计更干净的标注协议和 reward 特征。贡献量级偏分析型，不是新训练方法。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者没有训练新模型，而是用 mixed-effects logistic regression 分析 pairwise preference。模型形式接近 Bradley-Terry：把两个回答之间的偏好作为二分类因变量，把 citation 相关特征作为固定效应，并引入随机效应来处理样本分组带来的相关性。这样做的好处是可以把“同一个问题下不同回答的比较”与全局 citation 特征影响拆开，避免只看简单相关。论文关注的 citation 特征包括：Shannon entropy 衡量的 citation diversity、citation count、SciArena 中的 citation age，以及 ResearchQA 中的 citation mismatch。作者还让四个开源 LLM judge 对同一批 pairwise 样本打分，并对调回答顺序控制 position bias。与常见的 LLM-as-a-judge 论文不同，这里重点不是提高 judge 准确率，而是解释 judge 的偏好结构，并比较它和人类偏好的差异。","experiments":"实验使用两个科学问答偏好数据集。SciArena 有 12,249 个 human pairwise comparisons；ResearchQA 可用比较较小，只有 169 个。LLM judge 包括 Llama3.3-70B、Gemma3-27B、Qwen3-30B 和 Skywork-Critic-70B。为控制展示顺序，每个 pair 做两个 response ordering，因此每个 LLM 在 SciArena 上产生 24,498 次判断，在 ResearchQA 上产生 338 次判断。主要发现有三点：第一，人类更偏好 citation 来源更分散的回答，但并不偏好多引用，甚至倾向于较少引用。第二，LLM judge 对 citation 特征和非 citation 特征的权重与人类不同，有些 citation 相关偏好更强。第三，不同 LLM judge 之间的相关模式差异明显，专门训练为 reward model 的 Skywork-Critic 与通用模型并不只是能力强弱关系，而是偏好结构也不同。由于 ResearchQA agreement。Appendix 中的 judge prompt 也值得扫一眼，因为 citation 偏好很可能受到 prompt 中“引用可靠性”措辞影响。最值得深挖的是：如果把这类 citation 特征显式加入 reward model 或 judge calibration，是否能减少 RAG 场景下的格式奖励和引用堆砌。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how citations affect pairwise preferences in scientific QA. The central question is not whether an answer is factually correct, but whether interpretable citation features such as citation count, citation diversity, citation age, and citation mismatch systematically shift human and LLM-judge preferences. This matters because preference labels for RAG and research-assistant systems may encode citation-format biases rather than grounded answer quality.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is useful for understanding post-training data quality and reward modeling. Citations can act as a confounder in preference data: a reward model may learn that certain citation patterns look credible instead of learning to reward faithful grounding. The paper’s contribution is analytical. It turns citation behavior from a presentation issue into a measurable preference-bias issue. The main relevance is for citation-aware reward models, LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, and data collection protocols for deep-research assistants.","technical_approach":"The authors use mixed-effects logistic regression for pairwise preference analysis. The setup follows a Bradley-Terry-style view of binary comparisons: the dependent variable is which response wins, while citation-related variables are predictors. Mixed effects are used to handle grouped structure in the data rather than relying on flat correlations. Citation diversity is measured with Shannon entropy over cited sources; citation count captures the total number of citations; citation age is used for SciArena; citation mismatch is used for ResearchQA. The authors then run four open-weight LLM judges on the same examples, with both response orders, to control for presentation-order bias. The main distinction from typical LLM-as-a-judge work is that the paper focuses on explaining the judge’s preference structure rather than maximizing judge accuracy.","experiments":"The analysis uses two scientific QA preference datasets. SciArena contains 12,249 human pairwise comparisons. ResearchQA is much smaller, with 169 available comparisons. The LLM judges are Llama3.3-70B, Gemma3-27B, Qwen3-30B, and Skywork-Critic-70B. Each pair is judged under both response orderings, producing 24,498 judgments per LLM on SciArena and 338 per LLM on ResearchQA. The main findings are: humans tend to prefer more diverse citations but fewer citations overall; LLM judges weight citation and non-citation features differently from humans, sometimes with stronger citation-related preferences; and different LLM judges show distinct correlational patterns rather than a simple capability ordering. The ResearchQA results should be read cautiously because the sample size is small.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 minutes. We define \\emph{safety hacking} as selecting an output that passes the learned constraint but violates the true safety cr","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22915.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在推理阶段使用 Best-of-N (BoN) 采样并结合安全过滤器时，不完美的安全代理和奖励最大化机制会产生复合失效，导致模型最终选择看似安全实则违规的输出（Safety Hacking）。","importance":"揭示了推理期扩展（Inference-time Scaling）中一个被忽视的系统性风险。证明了仅靠提高安全过滤器的平均准确率无法解决问题，当不安全输出的奖励长尾分布比安全输出更重时，随着采样数 N 的增加，Safety Hacking 几乎必然发生。","technical_approach":"论文没有提出新的模型架构，而是建立了一个理论框架来分析 Constrained Best-of-N (CBoN) 采样中的 Safety Hacking 现象。作者将失效机制拆解为两步：1）污染（Contamination）：安全过滤器错误地放过了部分不安全输出；2）放大（Amplification）：奖励模型在候选集中倾向于给这些“漏网之鱼”打高分。通过推导有限 N 下的边界，证明了当不安全且被放过的输出具有更重的奖励上尾（upper reward tail）时，随着 N 增大，选择不安全输出的概率趋向于 1。此外，论文还分析了基于 KL 或 $\\chi^2$ 散度正则化的悲观采样（Pessimistic Sampling）策略，指出虽然正则化能限制放大效应，但无法修复已被污染的候选集。","experiments":"通过 Toy 实验和语言模型实验验证了理论。实验精确分解了有限 N 下的失效过程，分离了“暴露于不安全输出的概率”和“不安全输出得分超过所有安全输出的概率”。结果表明，后者随 N 增加而显著上升，是驱动 Safety Hacking 的核心因素。奖励模型的消融实验进一步确认了奖励排序在放大效应中的关键作用。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Introduction 中对 Safety Hacking 机制的两阶段拆解（污染与放大），以及理论部分关于奖励长尾分布如何决定渐近失效的推导。这对于理解为何单纯堆砌推理算力（增大 N）可能适得其反具有重要启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In inference-time pipelines using Best-of-N (BoN) sampling with a safety filter, the composition of an imperfect safety proxy and reward maximization creates a two-stage failure, leading the model to select outputs that pass the learned constraint but violate true safety criteria (Safety Hacking).","importance":"Exposes a systemic risk in inference-time scaling. It proves that improving the average accuracy of the safety filter is insufficient; if unsafe outputs have a heavier reward tail than safe ones, Safety Hacking becomes asymptotically certain as the sample size N increases.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a theoretical framework to analyze Safety Hacking in Constrained Best-of-N (CBoN) sampling rather than proposing a new architecture. The failure mechanism is decomposed into two stages: 1) Contamination, where the safety filter falsely admits unsafe outputs, and 2) Amplification, where the reward model assigns higher scores to these false positives. By deriving finite-N bounds, the authors show that when unsafe-but-feasible outputs possess a heavier upper reward tail, the probability of selecting an unsafe output approaches 1 as N grows. Furthermore, the paper analyzes pessimistic sampling regularized by KL or $\\chi^2$ divergence, noting that while regularization limits amplification, it cannot repair a contaminated feasible set.","experiments":"The theory is validated through toy and language model experiments. Consequently, later states may return to restricted knowledge regions af","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_23034.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的测试时遗忘（Test-Time Unlearning）方法多采用孤立的逐点激活编辑，忽略了自回归生成过程中隐藏状态的连续性，导致局部修正后，后续生成的 token 仍可能重新进入受限知识区域（Restricted Knowledge Re-entry）。","importance":"指出了当前测试时遗忘方法在长文本生成中的一个关键缺陷，并提出了一种基于轨迹控制的解决方案，显著降低了受限知识的重现率，为无需微调的模型安全对齐提供了更可靠的路径。","technical_approach":"提出了一种名为 ST$^2$U (Stateful Test-Time Unlearning) 的状态轨迹控制框架。核心机制包括：1）受限知识几何（Restricted Knowledge Geometry）：将目标相关的激活映射到低维可逆坐标中，通过密度比估计连续的风险边界，并保留与目标知识正交的成分；2）状态边界控制（Stateful Boundary Control）：在生成过程中持续监控风险，通过法向下降将高风险状态拉回边界以下，并将修正锚定到与上下文兼容的参考点以限制语义漂移；3）固定维度的修正状态：记录历史修正信息，并在当前边界的切空间内起作用，保持时间一致性的同时不阻碍风险降低。","experiments":"在 WMDP、RWKU 和 MUSE-Books 三个基准测试上进行了评估。ST$^2$U 实现了 80.6%–87.9% 的遗忘率，同时保留了 90.7% 的非目标能力。最关键的是，它将受限知识重现率限制在 13.76%–19.84%，而领先的测试时基线方法则高达 46.50%–59.10%。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Problem Formulation 中对“受限知识重现（Restricted Knowledge Re-entry）”的定义，以及 ST$^2$U 如何通过引入状态（Stateful）和轨迹级控制来解决这一问题。这对于理解自回归模型在干预下的动态行为很有帮助。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing test-time unlearning methods rely on isolated pointwise activation editing, ignoring the continuous nature of hidden states in autoregressive generation. This leads to \"Restricted Knowledge Re-entry,\" where subsequent tokens revert to restricted knowledge regions despite successful local corrections.","importance":"Identifies a critical flaw in current test-time unlearning methods during long-text generation and proposes a trajectory-control solution that significantly reduces the re-entry rate of restricted knowledge, offering a more reliable path for safe model alignment without fine-tuning.","technical_approach":"Proposes ST$^2$U (Stateful Test-Time Unlearning), a stateful trajectory-control framework. Key mechanisms include: 1) Restricted Knowledge Geometry: Maps target-related activations into low-dimensional invertible coordinates, estimates a continuous risk boundary via density ratio, and preserves orthogonal components; 2) Stateful Boundary Control: Monitors risk during generation, moves risky states below the boundary via normal descent, and anchors corrections to context-compatible references to limit semantic drift; 3) Fixed-dimensional correction state: Summarizes past corrections and acts within the tangent space of the current boundary, maintaining temporal consistency without hindering risk reduction.","experiments":"Evaluated on WMDP, RWKU, and MUSE-Books benchmarks. However, widely used methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) suffer from reward-gradient misalignment, leading to inefficient and unstable optimization. Recent work addresses this issue by reformulating RL with verifiable rewards (","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21830.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章处理的是 GUI agent 的 RLVR 训练里一个更细粒度的问题：现有从 GRPO 转向 contrastive/classification 目标的方法，虽然缓解了 reward-gradient mismatch，但仍然把整条轨迹压成成功/失败二元标签，无法区分“成功但很冗长”和“失败但离成功很近”这两类在 GUI 交互里很关键的差异。","importance":"对预训练主线的直接相关性不算高，但它对 post-train RL 的目标设计有一个清楚的启发：如果可验证奖励只有 outcome-level 二值信号，可以在不回到高方差 policy gradient 的前提下，把轨迹质量信息编码进 contrastive 目标内部。这个思路对 tool-use、agentic reasoning、甚至代码执行类 RLVR 都有可迁移性。它的价值不在提出新架构，而在说明如何在保持分类式优化稳定性的同时，引入组内偏好结构。","technical_approach":"作者提出 LACL-GUI，一个 length-aware 的 contrastive RLVR 框架。出发点是 GUI 任务的轨迹质量不仅由最终是否成功决定，还由执行效率和失败接近度决定。方法上分两部分。对成功轨迹，作者引入长度偏好：更短的成功轨迹被赋予更强的优化压力，冗长但同样成功的轨迹被相对下调，以鼓励高效操作而不是无谓绕路。对失败轨迹，作者定义 divergence length，用来衡量一条失败轨迹在多长前缀上仍与参考成功轨迹保持一致；偏离得越晚，说明越接近成功，这类负样本应被更弱地压制，因为它们包含更有用的接近正确行为。关键设计是这些偏好不是通过改 reward label 或重新定义 success boundary 来做，而是通过 outcome-preserving、zero-mean 的 logit modulation 注入到 contrastive/classification 目标里。这样可以在正类内部和负类内部重排优化强度，同时不破坏原有成功/失败判别边界，也尽量保留 REAL 一类方法的梯度稳定性。论文的理论动机是：相比 GRPO 的 reward-weighted likelihood，分类式目标能避免正样本梯度错配和负样本梯度支配；而 LACL-GUI 进一步在这个稳定框架里加入轨迹级偏好，而不是回退到更脆弱的标量回报。","experiments":"实验在 OSWorld 上进行，骨干模型是 Qwen3-VL-Thinking 4B 和 8B。作者报告在 50 个 training iterations 内，8B backbone 上 LACL-GUI 相比 GRPO 提升 2.8% task success rate，相比 REAL 提升 2.7%。摘要给出的信息显示它在两个模型规模上都稳定优于基线，说明收益不是单一尺寸偶然现象。更值得注意的是，提升来自对同 outcome 轨迹的细粒度区分，而不是简单增加正样本权重。按方法设计推断，消融重点应包括：去掉成功长度偏好、去掉失败 divergence length、以及去掉 zero-mean modulation 后性能和稳定性的变化；这些部分如果在正文中展开，主要用来证明两个偏好信号都在起作用，并且没有破坏分类式 RLVR 的优化性质。就量级看，2%–3% 的 OSWorld 提升不算大，但在 GUI agent 这类高噪声环境里，如果训练轮数只有 50 iter，这个幅度是有意义的。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，把作者与 GRPO、REAL、CLIPO 这条线的关系理清：它不是再证明 GRPO 有问题，而是在 classification-based RLVR 之上继续加细粒度偏好。然后重点看方法部分里 success length 和 failure divergence length 的定义，以及 zero-mean logit modulation 的公式，这决定了它为什么能“加偏好但不改边界”。实验部分建议看主表和消融，尤其是与 REAL agents. Recent methods move away from GRPO to classification-style objectives to avoid reward-gradient mismatch, but they still collapse each trajectory into a binary success/failure label. That loses important structure: some successful trajectories are unnecessarily long, and some failed trajectories are much closer to success than others.","importance":"Its direct connection to core pretraining is limited, but it offers a useful post-training lesson: when supervision is only outcome-level and verifiable, one can still inject trajectory-quality information without going back to high-variance policy-gradient optimization. The gains are reported consistently at both scales, which suggests the effect is not tied to a single model size. The important point is that the improvement comes from distinguishing trajectories within the same outcome class rather than simply reweighting positives versus negatives. The most relevant ablations, judging from the method, are likely the removal of success-length preference, the removal of failure divergence-length preference, and tests around the zero-mean modulation design to verify that the added structure improves learning without destabilizing optimization. Benchmark。基本任务要求模型逐字复写一个中性句子，同时在 prompt 中插入内部指令，例如“在写的时候想 bread”或“不要想 bread”。只保留复写正确的 trial，以减少输出内容差异对激活的混淆。测量对象是 residual stream。对每个概念和层，作者用“Tell me about {concept}”最后一个 prompt token 的 residual，减去无关词 residual 均值，得到 concept direction。生成时每个 token 的 residual 会投影到该方向上，用未归一化 projection 衡量概念信号，这样同时保留方向和 residual norm 的变化。benchmark 包含六个维度：Engage、Suppress、Dial Rank、Temporal Control、Coverage、Layer We analyze it through the policy variance $\\sigma_\\pi^2(s)=\\operatorname{Var}_{a\\sim\\pi}[Q_{\\pi}(s,a)]$, which in a deterministic MDP is the sole source of return variance and is injected in discrete pulses at states we call critical forks. (i) Policy variance is a discovery budget: observing an action of advantage $c$ requires $\\Omega(c^2/\\sigma_\\pi^","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22467.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在长时序、终端奖励的语言模型推理/工具使用任务里，credit fork：σ^2_π(s)≥τ 的状态。\n\n围绕两个问题给出理论结果：\n1) Local discovery：在固定状态 s，要观察到 advantage≥c 的动作需要多少次从 π(·|s) 采样。作者用 Cantelli/Chebyshev 等不等式把“尾部出现概率”与 σ^2_π(s) 联系起来，得到 Ω(c^2/σ^2_π(s)) 级别的下界，并在经典 two-point fork（两动作、价值差最大）上达到紧性。\n2) Long-horizon estimation：一旦观察到候选好动作，要在长地平线下把它的优势估计到足够精确以驱动策略更新，样本复杂度随剩余地平线呈指数恶化（文中强调这是支持 local credit assignment 的核心动机）。\n\n此外给出一个“第一阶段几乎免费”的 fork 筛选：用 next-token 分布的 Gini–Simpson dispersion 𝒟(s)=1-||π(·|s)||_2^2 上界 σ^2_π(s)（σ^2_π(s)≤𝒟(s)），因此只看一次前向得到的分布就能排除大量不可能是 critical fork 的状态；但该条件不充分（分布很散但动作等价值时 σ^2 仍可为 0），需要 rollout/critic 进一步确认。并讨论向随机环境扩展时哪些结论仍成立、哪些依赖确定性。","experiments":"全文内容以理论推导为主，核心“数字”是样本复杂度的量级与 tightness：在 canonical two-point fork 上，Cantelli 界在特定 c（如 c=1-ε）处可取等号，展示 Ω(c^2/σ^2) 下界的可达性；并给出 σ^2 与 𝒟 在 two-point policy C（论文强调这是解释性假设，不声称模型显式优化 C）。在这个视角下，组件承担四类操作角色：\n- Alignment：提出候选关联（例如把当前 token 与可能相关的上下文片段连起来）。\n- Unification：把相互支持的信息整合成更一致的解释。\n- Suppression：降低与当前解释不相容的备选项权重（典型联想到 copy-suppression/冲突消解）。\n- Routing：把被选中的信息稳定地送到输出位置/后续层。\n2）操作层：给出 attention head 的“角色签名（weight-space signatures）”与“激活层角色测度（activation-level role measures）”。核心思路是：不只在激活上做归因，而是尝试从权重结构中提取可泛化的统计特征，预测某个 head 更像 It frames the issue as representational geometry: whether hidden states for low-resource languages become less separable or use fewer effective directions. The main finding is a systematic relation between data availability and layer-wise geometry. The most consistent effect appears in the final layers, where low-resource languages show higher pairwise cosine similarity and stronger anisotropy, indicating more degenerate representation spaces.","importance":"The contribution is useful for pretraining and continued pretraining because it turns the low-resource language gap into a measurable internal phenomenon, not only a data-count problem. It suggests a practical workflow: diagnose degeneration by language and layer, then add geometry-aware regularization during CPT. This is relevant to multilingual data mixture design, low-resource adaptation, and representation monitoring during continual training. It also gives a cautionary signal: lower CPT loss does not necessarily mean healthier representations or better transfer. The paper is not a new large-scale multilingual training recipe; its value is mainly in diagnostics and lightweight intervention.","technical_approach":"The study has two parts. First, the authors analyze geometry across 30 languages. They use character counts in MADLAD-400 as a proxy for web-scale language availability and divide languages into five tiers, from very high-resource to very low-resource. They evaluate 9 open models: Llama 3.2 1B/3B, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 3 1B/4B/12B, and Qwen 3 1.7B/4B/8B. FLORES sentences are passed through each model, and token hidden states are collected layer by layer. The two metrics are average pairwise cosine similarity, which measures local separability, and isotropy, which measures how uniformly the representation space uses its dimensions. Second, the authors test geometry-regularized monolingual CPT for 10 African languages. CosReg penalizes high pairwise cosine similarity and encourages more distinct token representations. I-STAR encourages higher isotropy and more uniform use of dimensions.","experiments":"The geometry analysis spans 30 languages and 9 models from 1B to 12B parameters. The robust pattern is that lower-resource languages exhibit more degenerate final-layer geometry, with higher cosine similarity and anisotropy. In the CPT experiments, the authors adapt 9 base LLMs to 10 African languages and evaluate on IrokoBench. Geometry regularization reduces the measured degeneration. CosReg gives marginal downstream improvements for larger models, including Llama 3.1 8B, Qwen 3 8B, and Gemma 3 4B/12B. The largest gains are reported on AfriMGSM, a difficult mathematical reasoning benchmark for under-resourced languages. CosReg performs better than I-STAR, suggesting that local separability is more important than global isotropy in these settings. The visible text does not include full numerical tables, so the performance result should be read as a modest gain rather than a large capability shift.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–40 minutes. Then inspect the layer-wise geometry figures, especially the final-layer trends. For the intervention part, read the exact objectives for CosReg and I-STAR, where the regularizers are applied, and how hidden states are sampled to control cost. In the results, focus on IrokoBench, AfriMGSM, and the dependence on model size. The useful follow-up questions are whether the regularizers improve tasks beyond improving geometry metrics, whether they hurt high-resource languages or cross-lingual sharing, and whether the same approach works under multilingual CPT rather than monolingual adaptation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"低资源 CPT 不是只加语料；最终层 degeneration 这个诊断值得复现到内部多语种 anneal 实验。","en":"Low-resource CPT is not just more text; the final-layer degeneration diagnostic is worth replicating in multilingual annealing runs."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Francois Meyer","Jan Buys"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multilingual-LLM","continued-pretraining","representation-geometry","low-resource-languages"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23358","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.23358","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.23476","title":"On the Threat Model of Weird Generalization and Emergent Misalignment","abstract":"Narrow fine-tuning on small, domain-specific datasets can produce broad and surprising changes in model behavior-a phenomenon called weird generalization (WG). Yet, it remains unclear what features of the fine-tuning data are necessary for WG to arise. Here, we address this question by investigating a range of plausibly relevant features, including dataset size, composition, language, presentation style, and novelty relative to a model's parametric knowledge. Further, since WG evaluations rely o","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_23476.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“WG/EM 是否存在”，而是更关键的威胁建模问题：什么样的窄域微调数据，才会稳定诱发 weird generalization 或 emergent misalignment。已有讨论往往把少量异常 fine-tune 数据能触发广泛行为漂移，当作一种普遍风险；这篇论文沿着全文实验把这个说法拆开，逐项检查数据规模、数据混合比例、语言、呈现方式、以及与预训练知识的重合度，看看哪些因素真正决定 WG 的出现，哪些只是表面相关。同时作者还检查 WG 评测本身是否稳健，因为这类现象通常依赖很小的一组 probing questions，测量噪声可能和现象本身一样大。","importance":"对预训练与 post-train 接口的意义，在于它把“少量窄域数据会把模型整体带偏”这个宽泛担忧，收缩成了一个更具体的条件化结论：WG/EM 更像是脆弱、依赖数据工程的现象，而不是常规 domain fine-tuning 下高概率发生的事故风险。这个判断很重要，因为它影响两类研究方向的优先级。第一，安全侧不该只盯着样本量，而要更关注数据组成、语言形式、以及数据是否在模型参数知识中已有锚点。第二，做 continual pretrain、mid-train、领域适配的人，可以更细地理解“行为漂移”并不由小数据量自动触发，真正危险的是某些高度结构化、与模型已有表示强耦合的数据分布。贡献量级不在于提出新算法，而在于把一个被广泛讨论的安全现象，从轶事式观察推进到更像 threat model characterization 的层面。","technical_approach":"方法上作者没有发明新的训练算法，而是做了系统性的干预实验。模型选了三个已知会出现 WG 的开源权重模型：Llama-3.1-70B、Qwen-2.5-32B、Qwen-2.5-72B。数据选了四个先前已知能诱发 WG/EM 的窄域数据集：Old Bird Names、Medical Terms、Harry Potter、Extreme Sports。然后围绕五个维度构造变体。\n\n第一是数据规模。作者把原始窄域数据按 20/40/60/80/100% 采样，部分数据集还补了 10% 或 90% 点，检查 WG 是否随样本量单调增强。第二是数据组成。把会诱发 WG 的窄域数据，与不会诱发 WG 的 Dolly instruction 数据按不同比例混合，测试“有毒模式”在混合语料中的占比阈值。第三是语言。把数据翻译成其他语言，检查 WG 是否依赖原始语言表面形式，还是能跨语言稳定迁移。第四是呈现方式。作者区分 direct vs. indirect presentation，核心是在不改变知识内容的前提下，改变样本是否以直接问答、显式风格示范的方式出现，测试模型究竟是在学内容映射，还是在学更高层的 persona/style 模式。第五是 novelty，也就是数据内容相对预训练知识是熟悉还是新颖。全文的关键发现之一是：如果微调数据调用的是模型预训练中已经见过、已经有参数化表示的知识，WG 更容易被激活；如果内容是更新奇、缺少预训练锚点的，广泛泛化反而更弱。\n\n评测上，作者沿用前作的开放式问题 probing，并用 GPT-5-mini 作为 judge，分别打 generalization consistency 和 coherence 分数。更重要的是，他们没有把单一问题集当成金标准，而是分析不同问题集合下 WG 测量的波动。这一步很关键，因为如果现象对 question set 高敏感，那么很多“出现了 WG”的结论，可能部分来自评测采样偏差，而不是模型内部形成了稳定的新行为模式。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个模型、4 个基础数据集。数据规模从极小样本到全量，Birds 原始规模 208 条，Medicine 1139 条，HP 137 条，Sports 6000 条；作者还做了跨数据集对齐样本量的比较，例如把 Medicine 和 Sports 也裁到 137 或 208 条，避免把“数据集类型差异”误判成“样本量差异”。\n\n从结果看，最稳定的结论有三点。第一，WG 对数据组成和语言比对数据规模更敏感。也就是说，增加样本数并不会稳定、线性地放大 weird generalization；但把会诱发 WG 的样本掺入普通 instruction 数据时，比例变化会显著影响现象强度。第二，使用模型预训练中更熟悉的知识内容时，WG 更明显。这说明很多 WG 不是从零学出一个新 persona，而更像是微调触发了预训练里已有但平时不显性的表示或行为回路。第三，WG 测量对评测问题集敏感，说明现有小规模 probe 的方差不低。\n\n摘要里已经给出总体结论：WG/EM 是 fragile forms of generalization，且 absent adversarial data engineering 时不太可能自然出现。结合全文叙述，作者的证据链主要来自不同干预条件下 generalization score 的明显波动，而不是某个单点数字的提升。对这篇论文，最该记住的不是某个 benchmark 分数，而是“size 不是主导变量，composition/language/novelty 才更像开关”。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，把作者要修正的 threat model 先抓住。然后重点看数据规模与数据组成两组实验，因为这两部分最直接回答“常规窄域微调是否危险”。如果时间有限，语言和 novelty LLM 预训练的连接点在 continual pretraining 和 post-train 后的持续更新。它强调不要只从参数变化、梯度范数或 replay 策略理解遗忘，而要直接看新更新在函数空间里是否能与需要保留的行为共存。对大模型场景，这对应几个实际问题：继续预训练时新语料是否会破坏已有能力，领域增量训练是否只是在有限验证集上被 shield 住，SFT/RL comparator，不会直接写入模型。随后，AFM 在函数空间里寻找这个更新中与受保护行为兼容的分量，并只把可接受的部分存入普通参数。论文把 persistent adaptation、finite deployed protection 和 population behavior 区分开：在有限样本上恢复受保护输出，不代表参数中真正存下了无约束学习的有效部分，也不代表未见分布上的保持。框架还包含受限的行为敏感度 sketch、多时间尺度 metaplastic 控制、task-free routing、有限函数 shield，以及对 dormant structure 的 function-preserving renewal。形式化部分给出 streamed-memory certificate、rank-plasticity frontier、pathwise retention、projected stationarity 和动态 regret 的特例。","experiments":"实验设计的重点是因果干预，而不是把一个 continual-learning 算法简单跑在若干任务序列上。作者从匹配的神经网络状态出发，主动调节 functional compatibility \\(\\kappa\\)，同时控制无保护学习机会和保留约束，然后测量在相同 retention requirement 下有多少新学习可以被持久保存。给出的内容显示，兼容性越高，可接受的 persistent path fraction 越大；理论证书中还给出在条件满足时 \\(\\widehat{\\lambda}\\ge\\eta\\) 的保证。论文同时报告了消融和执行分析，用于区分“有限点位保护成功”和“参数路径中真正存储了新能力”这两件事。提供的正文片段没有足够信息确认具体模型规模、数据规模和数值提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 functional compatibility、same-state no-protection continual-learning benchmark 的地方。若关注大模型持续预训练，可以重点记录三个问题：compatibility 能否用低成本 proxy 估计，函数空间保护能否扩展到海量 probe，有限 protected set 与 population retention 之间如何建立可靠联系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether persistent learning under a retention constraint is determined by the functional compatibility between the incoming learning signal and the behavior that must be preserved.","importance":"The connection to foundation-model training is continual pretraining and long-running model maintenance. The paper shifts the discussion away from only parameter drift, gradient norm, replay, or regularization, and toward whether a new update can coexist with protected behavior in function space. For large LMs, this maps to practical questions such as whether new corpora interfere with existing capabilities, whether domain-adaptive training merely passes a finite protection set, and whether skills acquired by SFT or RL survive later training. These are measured across all 28 transformer layers. A random-label probe control is included to reduce the chance that probe performance is merely a capacity artifact. Incompatibility among weight-generated covariance, gates, and backward sensitivities is quantified through three families of commutators: between gates and covariance, between sensitivities and covariance, and between average gradient outer products (AGOPs) and neural feature matrices (NFMs). AGOP 与 NFM 满足严格的幂律关系，而是退一步研究它们是否可交换，即是否能被同一组正交基同时对角化。核心问题因此变成：在非线性网络中，哪些机制导致 AGOP–NFM 不可交换，哪些机制会让这种不兼容在层间传播或衰减。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练的直接相关性较弱。这不是语言模型训练 recipe、数据或架构论文，而是一篇偏理论的深度网络几何分析。它的启发主要在两个层面：第一，它给出一种比“看特征协方差”更细的分析语言，把门控、协方差、反向敏感度之间的不兼容写成可分解的交换子项；第二，它提醒人们，很多经验上观察到的谱对齐并不是单一机制驱动，而是多种源项经过层间 transport 后的结果。若未来有人想分析 Transformer/MLP block 中 sensitivity-covariance 交换子经过权重矩阵的 congruence transport 后得到的像。这解释了为什么 feature 侧看起来可能更对齐：小奇异值方向上的不兼容会被压低，协方差零空间中的分量甚至会被消去。第二层关系是对内部交换子 [Q_l, P_l] 建立精确的逐层递推，把它分解成四个来源：下游 transport、相邻层不平衡、点态敏感度波动、以及非线性 energies，用带缓冲区的谱块局部化方式衡量不同协方差子空间之间的直接耦合，而不是只看全局交换子范数。这样可以把真正跨谱块的混合，与近简并特征值簇内部的旋转区分开。整体上，这篇文章的技术价值在于把“谱对齐”从一个经验现象，改写成一套可分解、可局部化、可递推分析的有限宽几何框架。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，论文主体更偏理论推导，实验应主要承担说明性角色，而不是大规模 说明全局范数可能掩盖了谱块之间的选择性失配。当前片段没有给出具体数据集、网络规模或数值结果，因此不宜虚构数字。就阅读目的而言，实验不是这篇文章最重要的部分，关键在于看清作者如何把若干已有经验观察统一到交换子框架里。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住两个定义：AGOP 是输出对输入或中间表征的平均梯度外积，NFM 是 W^T W sensitivity geometry become spectrally aligned in deep nonlinear networks, and how that alignment is generated, transported, disrupted, or selectively canceled at finite width. Instead of insisting on a strict power-law relation between AGOPs and NFMs, it studies the weaker and more structural condition that they commute, meaning they are simultaneously orthogonally diagonalizable. The central problem is therefore to identify the mechanisms that create or suppress AGOP–NFM non-commutativity in nonlinear networks.","importance":"Its direct relevance to LLM pretraining is limited. This is not a paper about language-model objectives, data, scaling, or training recipes. Its value is more conceptual: it offers a finer vocabulary for discussing how gates, covariances, and backward sensitivities interact, and it shows that observed spectral alignment is not a single phenomenon but the net result of several transported and partially canceling sources. If one wants to analyze feature geometry, gradient statistics, and gating structure in Transformer MLP blocks or other modern architectures, this framework may serve as a mathematical template. But it is still some distance away from actionable guidance for large-scale pretraining.","technical_approach":"$67","experiments":"From the provided text, the paper is primarily theoretical, with experiments likely serving an illustrative rather than benchmark-driven role. tokens，得分 6.50；Sonnet 约 640K tokens，得分 8.42。说明“更便宜的小模型 + agent 迭代”不一定更省。4）自验证能力明显分层：frontier 模型 demo run rate 为 100%，最小模型为 0%，弱模型甚至不会把自己生成的系统端到端跑通。评测方法上，自动 checker 与 LLM judge 互补，judge 的 constraint While effective, these additions introduce substantial latency and engineering overhead. Domain-Specific Language (DSL) of 15 ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22651.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 text-to-SQL 里两个经常被混在一起、但机制上不同的问题：一是 composition，即如何稳定地产生可执行、结构正确、长而嵌套的查询；二是 grounding，即自然语言里的概念如何对上真实数据库中的表、列、值和连接键。作者的判断是，很多复杂 pipeline 把这两类问题一起交给自由形式 SQL 生成，再靠 schema linking、retrieval、candidate refinement、verifier 去补救，结果是系统越来越重，延迟和工程复杂度越来越高，但收益并不总是成比例。论文要回答的是：如果把“生成 SQL”改成“在受限 typed DSL 上逐步构造关系代数程序”，再配一个很朴素的 ReAct 式交互循环，是否已经足够接近当前复杂系统的准确率，同时显著降低成本和时延。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，这不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的论文；但它对“受约束动作空间 + 环境反馈”这类后训练/agent 设计有很清楚的启发。第一，它说明在结构化程序生成任务里，很多错误不是模型不会推理，而是输出空间太自由，导致语法、schema 对齐、列名引用这些低层错误淹没了真正的语义推理。第二，它把 deployment 里的 latency/cost 拉回到一等指标，而不是只看 end accuracy。第三，它给出一个很干净的例子：不一定需要更强的 planner 或更多外部模块，先把 action space 设计对，往往比堆复杂链路更有效。对做 LLM post-train、tool-use、program synthesis、甚至 constrained decoding 的人，这个结论比 text-to-SQL 任务本身更有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心有两层。第一层是 typed DSL。模型不直接写 SQL，而是在一个只有 15 个关系操作的 DSL 上行动，其中 12 个是构造 Relation 的操作，3 个是只读 probing 操作。所有中间结果都绑定到一个 immutable Relation 抽象上，后续操作只能引用已有 handle。这样做的关键不是“换个语法”，而是把大量原本要到 SQL 执行时才暴露的问题，前移到 compile-time：未知表、未知列、列已被上一步投影掉、参数类型不对，都会在 DSL engine 里被显式拦下，并返回 typed error。第二层是 ReAct 式 think → act → observe 循环。模型先在 <think> 中规划，再一次性发出一批 JSON tool calls；engine 顺序执行，返回每一步的 handle、可用列、必要时的结果预览和 row count。若某一步失败，后续 batch 停止，模型在下一轮根据错误信息修正。作者把 grounding 主要交给这种交互式 probing：先 table_ref 候选表，再 probe_values 或 execute 看值分布、样例行、连接键是否合理，然后再决定 join、filter、aggregate。论文还加了 answer-shaping pass，把 trace 中的执行结果整理成 benchmark 需要的最终答案格式。和已有方法相比，它刻意去掉 schema linking、retrieved demonstrations、candidate generation、专门 verifier 等模块，强调“typed action space 解决 composition，iteration 解决 grounding”，而不是用更多组件把两者一起包起来。","experiments":"结果部分最重要的不是单点准确率，而是 accuracy、cost、latency 一起看。在 corrected BIRD mini-dev 上，ReAct-SQL 达到 84.5% accuracy；在 EHR-SQL 上达到 73.9%。作者的主张是，这个水平已经能和不少更复杂的系统接近，但成本和中位延迟更低。摘要和正文都强调是在 matched models、identical settings 下做端到端比较，避免因为底模不同而失真。论文还分析了失败模式：长查询和深嵌套场景里，typed DSL 明显减少了 hallucinated table/column 和语法错误；而在 schema 名称很不透明的数据库上，性能瓶颈更多来自 grounding，不是 DSL 本身能完全解决的。消融上，关键对比通常包括：直接 SQL 生成 vs DSL；单轮/少轮生成 vs iterative ReAct；是否使用 probing 工具；以及不同系统在每 query token/cost/latency 上的差异。作者的结论是，最近几类 text-to-SQL 系统在 accuracy 上已经比较接近，但在 cost 和 latency 上分化很大，这也是他们认为评测标准需要调整的原因。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 里对 composition / grounding 的拆分，这决定了整篇方法设计。然后看 Method 里 DSL 的定义、Relation abstraction、compile-time validation 和 到底帮了什么、又没帮什么。预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主要结论；若做 tool-use 或 constrained generation，值得把 DSL 设计和错误返回机制细读一遍。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper separates text-to-SQL errors into two different problems that are often conflated: composition and grounding. Composition is about producing a well-formed executable query, especially when the target SQL is long or nested. Grounding is about mapping the user question to the right tables, columns, values, and join keys in a specific schema. The authors argue that many modern systems handle both with free-form SQL generation plus a growing stack of schema linking, retrieval, candidate generation, and refinement modules. That stack can improve accuracy, but it also raises latency and engineering cost. The paper asks a narrower question: if SQL generation is replaced by a typed relational DSL, and grounding is handled through a simple iterative ReAct loop with execution feedback, is that already enough to reach competitive zero-shot performance?","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model researchers working on tool use, constrained generation, and post-training interfaces. The main lesson is that many failures in structured generation come from an overly permissive output space rather than from missing high-level reasoning. By shrinking the action space and validating actions before execution, the system removes a large class of low-level errors. A second contribution is methodological: the paper treats latency and cost as first-class metrics, not as afterthoughts behind execution accuracy. A third lesson is architectural simplicity. Instead of adding more planners and verifiers, the work shows that a carefully designed action space plus environment feedback can recover much of the benefit with a much lighter system.","technical_approach":"$6a","experiments":"The headline numbers are 84.5% accuracy on corrected BIRD mini-dev and 73.9% on EHR-SQL. The more important claim is that these results are obtained with a much simpler zero-shot pipeline and lower end-to-end cost and median latency under matched-model comparisons. The paper emphasizes that recent systems are often tightly clustered in accuracy while differing substantially in runtime overhead. The analysis also clarifies where the gains come from. The typed DSL reduces hallucinated tables and columns, syntax failures, and brittle long-query generation. Iterative probing helps when the schema is opaque and the model needs evidence from values or row previews before deciding how to map the question. The remaining hard cases are mostly grounding-heavy settings where schema names are uninformative. The ablations focus on DSL versus direct SQL generation, iterative interaction versus less interactive variants, and the contribution of probing tools to final accuracy and efficiency.","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction, especially the composition-versus-grounding split. 上的节点通过 clone/init_from 继承同一条 VM 血缘。\n- Branch 是并行分支，用于信息收集或可“提取”的产物生成（extractable deliverables，如文本答案、下载文件、导出数据）。Branch 可以从某个 spine VM 克隆出来或从 base image 启动，完成后只把可提取产物回传，然后丢弃 VM。\n- 允许 deliverables 形成任意依赖图，但 VM state 只沿 spine 单线流动，从构造上消除“合并 VM”的需求。\n- 协调上减少对 manager 在状态转移上的负担：状态沿 spine 传递，产物在依赖节点间直接传递。\n\n取舍很明确：把“不可复制的状态”串行化，保留并行只给“可复制的产物”。这牺牲了一部分并行度，但换来更少的状态丢失与重建成本。","experiments":"在 Odysseys 的 200 个 long-horizon 任务上，跨 3 个不同能力水平的 CUA backbone，Spine-Branch 相比基线提升成功率 6.0%–16.5%，并把单任务金融成本降低 34%–70%。作者还在 OSWorld 2.0 上做了补充实验，结论是多智能体协调通常不值得其成本，并给出 GUI 任务分解的实践原则。论文把收益归因到两点：分解更“干净且可并行”，以及 spine 保留昂贵的 live VM state，避免无约束并行带来的状态丢失与重建。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（20–30 分钟）：\n- 直接看 Introduction 对 single-parent VM inheritance 的定义与动机，这是全文最关键的“问题刻画”。\n- 看方法图（Figure 1）与方法小节里对 spine/branch、deliverables/VM state 的形式化定义，理解它为何从结构上避免 VM merging。\n- 快速扫实验主表：成功率提升与成本下降的幅度，以及在 hard/long-horizon 子集上的分层结果。\n- 如果你不做 CUA/GUI agent，可跳过实现细节与 OSWorld 的实践建议部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Multi-agent computer-use agents can parallelize subtasks across virtual machines, but there is a hard physical constraint: two diverged VM live states cannot be merged (single-parent VM inheritance). Prior systems handle this ad hoc, often discarding state or paying to reconstruct it. The paper addresses how to plan multi-agent execution so that parallelism is useful without ever requiring VM-state merging.","importance":"This is primarily an agent coordination / systems planning contribution, with limited direct relevance to core LLM pretraining (data, tokenizer, scaling, optimizer). Its main transferable idea is the explicit formalization of non-mergeable environment state as a first-class constraint and a decomposition pattern that respects it. It may be useful if you work on GUI-agent post-training data generation or evaluation design, but it is not a pretraining-centric must-read.","technical_approach":"Spine-Branch Coordination:\n- Represent a task as a typed dependency graph.\n- The spine is a serial chain that carries persistent VM state via cloning (init_from). All stateful operations that are expensive to rebuild stay on this single lineage.\n- Branches run in parallel on fresh VMs or clones of a spine VM, but are restricted to producing extractable deliverables (text, files, exported artifacts). Once done, branch VMs are discarded.\n- Deliverables can flow through an arbitrary dependency graph, while VM state flows along a single spine, eliminating the need for VM merging by construction.\n- Coordination is simplified: state transfer is implicit along the spine; artifacts are passed between dependent nodes.\nThe trade-off is deliberate: serialize non-copyable state, parallelize only copyable artifacts.","experiments":"On 200 long-horizon Odysseys tasks and across three CUA backbones, Spine-Branch improves success rate by 6.0%–16.5% and reduces per-task financial cost by 34%–70% versus a baseline. Additional experiments on OSWorld 2.0 suggest multi-agent coordination is often not cost-effective, and the paper distills practical decomposition principles. The analysis attributes gains to cleaner parallel decompositions and preservation of expensive live VM state on the spine, avoiding state loss and reconstruction overhead.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (20–30 minutes):\n- Introduction for the crisp definition and motivation of single-parent VM inheritance.\n- The main method figure and the formal distinction between extractable deliverables vs VM state; this is the key conceptual payload.\n- The main results table for success-rate and cost deltas, plus the breakdown on harder/longer-horizon tasks.\n- If you are not working on GUI agents, you can skip implementation details and most of the OSWorld practical guidance.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 §方法和失败案例；把不可合并环境状态显式建模，是长程 agent 里少见的干净系统约束。","en":"Read the method and failure cases; explicitly modeling non-mergeable environment state is a clean systems constraint for long-horizon agents."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mian Zhang","Manasi Sharma","Sheng Zhang","Minglai Yang","Kejian Shi","Ying Liu","Zhiyu Zoey Chen","Daniel Yue Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["computer-use-agents","multi-agent","long-horizon-tasks","parallelism","agent-systems"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22077","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22077","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22118","title":"RAG Collapse: LLM Responses Collapse When Retrieved Documents Are Self-Authored","abstract":"LLM responses are based on the internet (via training or RAG), and AI is now used to generate a significant amount of content online (Paredes et al., 2026), creating the potential for a self-reinforcing feedback loop. previously generated by the same model.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper. Its relevance is data feedback. The main lesson is that model-output feedback can affect systems before recursive retraining happens. If self-authored web content enters the retrieval context, the model may over-weight its own prior wording and perspective, causing answer diversity to shrink. For pretraining and continual-pretraining pipelines, this matters because the same failure mode can appear in synthetic data generation, auto-labeling, web refreshes, and retrieval-based data construction. It also suggests that provenance tracking and entropy-style monitoring should be part of data governance for both RAG and training corpora.","technical_approach":"The authors simulate AI systems retrieving content they previously generated. The workflow starts with an information-seeking question and a set of original references. The model samples multiple answers from those references. Some answers are then stripped of citations and rewritten as online articles. These self-authored articles either replace original references or enter a pool of retrievable documents. The model then answers the same question again under the modified context. The paper studies three settings: Replace All, Replace One, and Search. Replace All resembles recursive replacement. Replace One models gradual contamination. Search models self-authored documents being selected by a retrieval tool. Collapse is measured through paraphrase rates, entity visibility distributions, and citation behavior. A useful part of the design is the comparison between self-authored references and other AI-generated references, including controls for reference quality.","experiments":"The experiments use three model families, 1,019 information-seeking prompts, and 1,528 simulations. RAG collapse appears in 79.6% of simulations, or 1,216 out of 1,528. In one reported setting, the fraction of response pairs that are paraphrases increases from 22% at the beginning to 89% by the end. Collapse often starts early, sometimes after introducing only a single self-authored reference. The paper also finds that self-authored references are cited more often than original references and have stronger influence than other AI-generated references. This remains after controlling for reference quality, which supports a self-bias explanation. The Twitch streamer example illustrates the mechanism clearly: initially variable entity mentions converge to 0% or 100% visibility, so the system loses diversity and changes which entities survive.","reading_guide":"A 25–30 minute skim is appropriate. Read the Introduction and Example first to understand the phenomenon. Then focus on Section 4, where the three simulations are defined; this is the key section for judging external validity.
