The main caveats are that the simulated publishing and retrieval loop is still simpler than the open web, and that collapse measures diversity loss rather than factual accuracy directly. The failure mode is still relevant for RAG design, continual pretraining, and synthetic-data feedback loops.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改预训练方法，但会改你对“AI 语料污染只影响训练集”的 prior；先看 collapse 定义和单篇自引触发那组实验，若判据干净，数据治理和 RAG 去污染都得前移。","en":"It does not change pretraining recipes directly, but it should update your prior that AI contamination is only a training-data problem; read the collapse definition and the single-self-authored-reference result first."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Gregory Druck","Ethan Smith"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RAG","model-collapse","self-generated-data","retrieval","data-contamination"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22118","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22118","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22152","title":"The Collaboration Tax: How Much LLM Multi-Agent Systems Pay to Coordinate","abstract":"Multi-agent systems built from large language models are deployed widely, yet how much performance is lost when two LLMs must coordinate rather than act alone remains unclear. We formulate the collaboration tax as the team-decentralisation loss of a two-player cooperative game with private information, with two propositions characterising its sign and its equivalence to a max-superadditivity violation. We operationalise this definition on 32 solo-tractable tasks grouped by source of grounding fr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22152.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“multi-agent 有没有用”，而是一个更基础的问题：当两个 LLM 必须在私有信息条件下协作完成一个原本单模型可解的任务时，协作本身到底会带来多大损失，以及损失来自哪里。作者把这个损失形式化为 collaboration tax，即 full-information solo 与 split-view paired execution 之间的成功率差。关键点在于任务被设计成 solo-tractable，因此性能下降不能归因于任务本身太难，而更接近于去中心化协作带来的信息传递、对齐和验证失败。全文真正有价值的地方，是把“协作失败”从泛泛的 agent 现象，拆成可测量、可分解、可干预的机制问题。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于提出了新的预训练方法，而在于它给出了一个较干净的 coordination failure 测量框架。这个框架对 post-train agent 系统有用，但对 pretrain 也有启发：如果模型在单体推理上已经足够强，而在私有视角协作中仍稳定失分，那么瓶颈更可能在 grounded communication、信息请求策略、证据整合和结果复核这些能力上，而不是纯 reasoning depth。换句话说，这篇论文提示了一个常被忽略的能力维度：模型是否学会在不完整视角下进行高效的信息交换。这个维度未来可以反哺预训练数据设计、对话式训练目标、以及多角色自博弈式 continued pretraining。贡献量级偏“评测与机制分析”，不是范式级方法论文，但问题定义清楚，实验组织也比较扎实。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层。第一层是形式化定义。作者借用 team decentralisation 和 cooperative game 的视角，把 collaboration tax 定义为：同一任务上，拥有全部信息的单模型成功率，与两个各持部分私有信息的协作系统成功率之间的差值。文中给了两个命题来说明这个量的符号条件，以及它与 cooperative game 中 superadditivity 失效的关系。这个理论部分不复杂，但作用是把“协作不如单干”从经验观察变成了一个有明确参照系的量。\n\n第二层是任务构造。作者做了 32 个任务，分成 Spatial、Relational、CSP 三类，分别对应不同的 grounding friction：空间参照系对齐、词汇/类别对齐、索引与顺序对齐。任务都刻意做成单模型在 full information 下可解，然后再把信息拆给两个 agent。这个设计比很多 agent benchmark 更干净，因为它尽量排除了工具调用、长链规划、环境反馈等混杂因素，专门测 coordination cost。\n\n第三层是机制分析与干预。作者把失败过程拆成四阶段 cascade：grounding、querying、integration、re-derivation。grounding 指模型先把双方都没说过的事实编出来；querying 指没有主动向对方索取缺失信息；integration 指拿到两边信息后没有先整合就直接作答；re-derivation 指接收方不重新推导，只是接受对方答案。论文进一步用 conversation-shape features 去回归预测 tax，得到一个很有意思的结论：模型能力更像设定了截距，决定总体上限；而对话形态决定斜率，决定协作时到底损失多少。最后作者做了一个纯 prompt-level 的 combined intervention，在 system prompt 里分别加入针对四阶段的约束条款，测试是否能在不改模型的情况下缩小 gap。这个设计朴素，但正因为朴素，才能支持作者的核心判断：问题主要出在协作过程，而不是底层推理能力不足。","experiments":"实验覆盖 11 个模型、7 家提供方，并同时测了 solo、同构双人、异构双人以及 slot-swapped heterogeneous 配置。摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但从全文描述可以确定几个稳定结论。第一，collaboration tax 在所有模型和任务族上都不是偶发现象，而是结构化存在，并且有两个“无例外排序”：任务类别上，某些 grounding friction 明显更难；模型能力上，能力越强 tax 越低，而且是单调下降。第二，失败不是主要由 reasoning deficit 驱动。作者通过对话标注和特征分析发现，很多失败发生在拿到足够信息之前，或者拿到信息之后没有整合和复核。第三，异构配对里损失主要由强模型承担，也就是弱模型不仅不能稳定补足信息，反而会把强模型拖入低质量协作轨道。第四，prompt intervention 能回收相当一部分 tax，而且 leave-one-out ablation 显示不同任务族的主瓶颈不同：有的更缺 querying，有的更缺 integration。这一点比“统一加长 prompt 就变好”更有信息量，因为它说明 tax 不是单一噪声源，而是任务结构相关的失配。\n\n如果从实验设计质量看，优点是控制变量比较严，特别是 solo-tractable 这一点让解释更可信。局限也很明显：任务规模仍偏小，主要是双人协作，且没有把工具使用、外部记忆、显式协议学习纳入比较，因此结论更适用于“自然语言对话式协作”而不是所有 multi-agent 系统。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看问题定义、任务设计和四阶段机制，不必在 cooperative game 的形式化上停留太久。优先看 Figure 1 把 tax 和四阶段 cascade 建立直觉；再看任务分类部分，理解 Spatial、Relational、CSP 为什么能代表不同的 grounding friction；然后看主要结果图，确认 tax 的两个排序是否真的稳；最后看 intervention 和 leave-one-out ablation，这部分最接近可操作结论。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主线。如果只想判断这篇论文是否值得进入自己的 agent/pretrain 交叉问题清单，看 introduction、task construction、mechanism analysis、prompt intervention 四部分就够了。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a clean coordination question rather than a generic multi-agent one: when two LLMs must solve a task under split private information, and the same task is solvable by a single model with full information, how much performance is lost purely because of collaboration? The collaboration tax is defined as the success gap between the full-information solo setting and the split-view paired setting. The important design choice is that tasks are solo-tractable by construction, so the observed gap is not mainly about raw task difficulty. It is about decentralised coordination failure: sharing the right facts, asking for missing information, integrating partial views, and verifying the final answer.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for foundation-model research because it isolates a capability axis that standard reasoning evaluations often miss. If strong models still lose reliably once information is partitioned across agents, the bottleneck is not just reasoning depth. It is grounded communication under partial observability. That matters for agent systems today, and it also suggests future pretraining directions: dialogue data that rewards information-seeking, objectives that train explicit evidence integration, or continued pretraining setups with role-split self-play. The contribution is best read as a measurement and mechanism paper. It gives a sharper language for coordination failure than the usual “multi-agent sometimes helps, sometimes hurts” narrative.","technical_approach":"$6b","experiments":"$6c","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on the problem definition, task construction, and the four-stage failure cascade. posi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22610.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是技能库型自演化 Agent 的可靠性问题：一个新技能被加入 skill bank 后，整体任务分数变好，并不代表这个技能本身在后续推理中有正贡献。技能的效用会受到已有技能组合和部署域的影响。","importance":"对 LLM 基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 后的长期记忆、经验蒸馏、Agent 自演化系统有参考价值。论文把 skill bank 从“可复用经验集合”重新看成一个有组合效应的动态系统，指出 outcome-only 的接纳门控无法识别技能之间的相互作用。这对持续学习、记忆库维护、工具/技能选择都有启发：不能只看加入某个样本或模块后的 aggregate reward，还要看它在不同上下文组合里的边际贡献。","technical_approach":"论文先提出 Skill Mechanistic Reliability coalition-aware 信号，通过 Monte Carlo 采样不同技能子集，并估计 Shapley marginal contribution，把组合条件下的可靠性与 aggregate reward 一起用于接纳决策。针对后者，u-SMCO 使用目标域无标签 query，根据技能对 retrieval quality 的贡献进行打分，贪心 mask 掉移除后能改善检索的技能。方法的重点不是改 LLM 参数，而是改技能库维护和部署时的选择机制。","experiments":"实验覆盖 LoCoMo、LongMemEval、HotpotQA 和 ALFWorld，分别对应长程记忆、多跳问答和具身交互等场景。论文报告 CASS 和 u-SMCO 相比技能型自演化基线在任务表现和跨域泛化上有稳定提升。审计部分的主要现象是：新加入技能的正向 aggregate reward 不能说明它有独立贡献；已有技能的边际贡献会随技能组合变化；迁移到目标域后，一些源域正贡献技能会出现负贡献。提供内容中没有可核验的具体提升数值，因此不宜给出精确百分比。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Skill Mechanistic Reliability Audit 的设计、coalition pollution 设定，但可以借鉴它对“数据/技能加入是否真有边际贡献”的审计思路。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies reliability in skill-bank-based self-evolving agents. A skill can improve aggregate task performance when added to a bank, but that does not imply the skill itself makes a positive mechanistic contribution during later deployment.","importance":"The work is not a direct contribution to foundation-model pretraining, but it is relevant to post-training systems that use persistent memory, distilled experience, or reusable skills. Its main lesson is that a memory or skill bank should be treated as an interacting set, not as a list of independently useful artifacts. This is also a useful analogy for continual learning and data selection: aggregate gain after adding an item can hide weak or negative marginal utility under different coalitions.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces Skill Mechanistic Reliability Audit, which varies skill-bank composition and deployment domains to measure how individual skills affect agent behavior. It identifies two failure modes. Coalition pollution occurs when a newly accepted skill has near-zero contribution, while an incumbent skill carries the bank’s performance. Cross-domain utility reversal occurs when a skill that helps in the source domain hurts after transfer. CASS addresses the first issue by estimating coalition-conditioned reliability with Monte Carlo sampled Shapley marginal contributions and combining that signal with aggregate reward for skill acceptance. u-SMCO addresses the second issue by using unlabeled target-domain queries to score each skill’s retrieval contribution and greedily masking skills whose removal improves retrieval.","experiments":"The experiments use LoCoMo, LongMemEval, HotpotQA, and ALFWorld, covering long-memory tasks, multi-hop QA, and embodied interaction. The paper reports consistent gains from CASS and u-SMCO over skill-based self-evolving baselines, especially under skill evolution and cross-domain transfer. The audit results show that accepted skills may have little direct value, that incumbents’ marginals shift with the surrounding bank, and that source-domain utility can become negative in the target domain. The provided content does not include exact improvement numbers, so precise percentages should be checked in the tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the audit setup, the examples of coalition pollution and utility reversal, and the algorithmic details of CASS and u-SMCO. The benchmark-specific sections are less important for pretraining researchers. The most reusable idea is the audit protocol: evaluate the marginal contribution of a reusable artifact under multiple coalitions and deployment domains, rather than accepting it based only on aggregate reward.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得读审计设计；它提醒 agent memory/skill 不是数据越多越好，负迁移要按组合而非单技能测。","en":"Read the audit setup; it usefully shows that agent skill memory is not monotonic with more data, and negative transfer must be measured at the coalition level."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Qiyan Zhao","Xiaofeng Zhang","Bo Liu","Minda Chen","Wei Xiong","Jingyang Chen","Guanting Ye","Wenhao Yu","Xiaosong Yuan","Shijie Han","Da-Han Wang","Jianmin Ji","Fei Huang","Xu-Yao Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["LLM-agents","self-evolving-agents","skill-bank","reliability-audit","negative-transfer"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22610","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22610","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22752","title":"The Compaction Cliff in Long-Running AI Agent Memory","abstract":"A safety rule and an episodic log compete for the same tokens in an AI agent's context. When the budget overflows, both are summarized at the same rate; only the rule needs exact wording to remain enforceable. On 20 production agent configurations, Claude Code's /compact prompt on Sonnet 4.6 preserves 53\\% of safety rules after one compaction round and 10\\% after five. We name this the Compaction Cliff. We address it with Knowledge Triage, a framework that classifies each line of an agent's know","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22752.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.IR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长会话 agent 在有限上下文预算下的记忆保真问题，尤其是“安全规则”和“普通经历日志”被同一种压缩策略对待时，会出现规则措辞被改写、限定条件丢失、最终失去可执行性的结构性失败。作者把这个现象定义为 Compaction Cliff：在多轮 compaction 后，真正需要逐字保真的约束类知识，比可容忍摘要化的背景信息衰减得更快。论文进一步指出，这不是单一 prompt 写得不够好，而是现有 production agent 常见的 type-blind memory management 在 compaction、decomposition、retrieval 三个操作上都没有区分知识类型，因此无法同时满足不同知识的保真要求。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于提出了新的 LLM 训练算法，而在于把“上下文内知识管理”从 prompt engineering 提升成了一个有类型约束的表示与保真问题。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这篇文章有两个启发。第一，长程 agent 的失败不只是检索不到，而是表示被错误压缩；这和训练时如何让模型区分 rule-like knowledge、procedure-like knowledge、episodic trace 有关系。第二，很多 agent memory 方案默认“摘要就是压缩”，但这篇文章说明不同知识需要不同失真模型，这对未来做 memory-aware pretraining、typed synthetic data、或专门的 verifier/objective 都有参考价值。贡献量级偏系统与问题定义，不是范式级方法，但问题刻画比较扎实。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Knowledge Triage。作者先从 396,934 个 agent knowledge artifacts 中抽象出五类知识类型，并主张这是能支撑后续安全论证的最粗有效划分。文中符号里类型集合写作 {C, P, B, F, E}，结合正文语义，大致对应约束/规则、过程/操作步骤、背景信念或项目知识、事实性配置/命令片段、以及 episodic logs 一类。关键不是命名，而是每类允许的失真不同：约束类要求 wording-level fidelity，过程类要求 behavior-preserving rewrite，日志类可以高度摘要甚至丢弃。\n\n在表示上，知识库被建模为 K=(I, τ, T, π, σ)：I 是条目集合，τ 是类型分类器，T 是 topic tree，π 把条目映射到 topic leaf，σ 给约束类条目定义作用域。这个结构让作者把三个常见上下文管理操作统一起来：\n1. TypeCompact：在预算内原地压缩，但按类型使用不同 fidelity policy，而不是统一 summarization。\n2. TypeDecompose：当某个 topic 大到无法安全压缩时，先按 topic/subtopic 拆分，再分别处理，避免把不可压缩的规则和可压缩的背景揉在一起。\n3. TypeRetrieve：把外存检索也做成 typed retrieval，不只是相关性排序，还要考虑哪些类型必须优先回填。\n\n作者还加了两个配套部件。一个是 post-compaction guard verifier，用来检查压缩后关键 guard 是否仍然可执行；另一个是 SafetyMargin classifier，它不是按表面语法判断“像不像安全规则”，而是按 counterfactual safety 评分：删掉或改写某条内容后，是否会显著改变 agent 的安全边界。这个设计比关键词匹配更接近实际风险。\n\n论文的一个重要点是，它不把问题停留在经验观察，而是给出 per-operator safety requirement，并论证 type-blind 策略在三类操作上都无法满足这些要求。也就是说，失败不是某个模型不够强，而是单一保真度策略与异质知识对象之间存在不相容性。","experiments":"实验分两层。第一层是现象复现。作者在 20 个 production agent configurations 上测 Claude Code 的 /compact prompt 配合 Sonnet 4.6，发现安全规则在一次 compaction 后只保留 53%，五轮后降到 10%。引言里还提到在 50 个真实 agent configurations 上，hierarchical truncation 只能保留约 50% 的安全约束。作者把这种快速坍塌称为 Compaction Cliff。更重要的是，这个现象不只出现在单一模型上，而是在四类 LLM compactor family 和一个最强的非 LLM compressor 上都能复现，说明问题具有结构性。\n\n第二层是框架验证。作者构建了 AgentArtifactCorpus，规模为 396,934 个 artifacts，来自 54,628 个公开 GitHub 仓库，并做了 2,000 条人工类型标注。五类知识覆盖了 97% 的真实配置内容，这个数字支撑了 typed model 的实用性。随后在五个公开语料上做 end-to-end validation，验证 typed triage、guard verifier、SafetyMargin classifier 的组合能比统一压缩更好地保留安全约束，同时不显著牺牲预算利用率。给到的全文片段没有完整表格数字，因此无法逐项复述所有提升幅度，但从作者的叙述看，主要收益集中在 constraint recall 和多轮 compaction 稳定性，而不是一般摘要质量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 3，把 Compaction Cliff 的定义、测量方式和衰减曲线看清楚。这里决定了这篇文章值不值得继续读。然后看 §3 的 typed knowledge model，重点不是记符号，而是理解为什么作者坚持把 compaction、decomposition、retrieval 视为同一个预算分配问题，以及为什么单一 fidelity policy 在三种操作上都会出错。接着看 §4.2 和 Table 2，确认五类知识划分是否足够自然、覆盖率是否站得住。最后看 guard verifier 和 SafetyMargin classifier 的实现细节，判断它们是工程 heuristics 还是可迁移的方法。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟内建议读：Introduction、Table 2、Figure 3、实验主表。若要深挖，值得追问三点：第一，类型分类是否依赖特定 agent artifact 分布，迁移到更开放的 tool-use agent 是否还成立；第二，SafetyMargin 的 counterfactual 标注如何构造，是否会引入新的主观偏差；第三，这套 typed memory 能否前移到训练阶段，变成数据标注或辅助目标，而不只是 runtime patch。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in long-running agent memory under a fixed context budget. Safety rules and ordinary episodic content are often compacted with the same summarization policy, even though they have very different fidelity requirements. The result is what the authors call the Compaction Cliff: constraints that require near-verbatim preservation lose enforceability after repeated compaction, because qualifiers, scope, or exact wording are paraphrased away. The paper argues that this is not merely a prompt-quality issue. It is a structural consequence of type-blind memory management across the three standard context operations: compaction, decomposition, and retrieval.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it reframes agent memory as a typed representation and fidelity-allocation problem rather than a generic summarization problem. For researchers working on the pretrain/post-train boundary, the main value is diagnostic. It shows that long-horizon agent failures can come from lossy rewriting of rule-like knowledge, not only from retrieval misses. It also suggests that future work on memory-aware pretraining, typed synthetic corpora, or verifier-based objectives may need to distinguish constraints, procedures, and episodic traces explicitly. The contribution is mainly in problem formulation, empirical characterization, and systems design, not in a new LM training paradigm.","technical_approach":"$6e","experiments":"$6f","reading_guide":"$70","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看实验设置和安全规则保留曲线；它把“摘要压缩够用”这个 Agent prior 打得很具体。","en":"Read the setup and the safety-rule retention curve; it gives a concrete counterexample to the usual “summarization is enough” memory prior."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Saber Zerhoudi","Jelena Mitrovic","Michael Granitzer"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["agent-memory","context-compaction","safety","long-horizon-agents"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22752","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.22752","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.22631","title":"Learning Generalizable Behaviors for Terminal Agents","abstract":"Terminal agents are a compelling application of large language models (LLMs), with the potential to integrate deeply into users' daily workflows. Existing work main","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_22631.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在合成 unified","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21918.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文试图解决自动化多智能体系统设计中的一个具体问题：搜索式
